| `elements::pos_seq::legs::tests::the_legs_of_every_connection` | wye three phases (`(0,3) (1,3) (2,3)`), wye one phase (`(0,1)`), delta three phases (`(0,1) (1,2) (2,0)`), delta one and two phases (`(0,1)`, `(0,1) (1,2)`), per conductor to earth of three conductors (`(0,earth) (1,earth) (2,earth)`, the VCCS), a branch of three conductors (`(0,3) (1,4) (2,5)`), a Vsource with terminal 2 on earth (no leg), and the `v_leg` of a 12.47 kV wye and delta element (7199.56, 12470 V), and `pos_seq_pattern` of a three-phase Vsource, a two-phase Isource and a Load | the pairs and voltages listed, the patterns `(1, a², a)`, `(1, a²)` and `None` | T1 | section 1.2 |

**Divergences.** `modes:makeposseq/makeposseq_pc_repeat.dss` on `capi_v0145` and on the props channel: its second `makeposseq` no longer divides Load `kw`, `kvar`, `xfkva` by 3 again and the Vsources' `basekv` by √3 again. The planner measures the cells that move (voltages, powers, `y`, the props of `Load.ld_wye`, `Load.ld_delta`, `Vsource.source`, `Vsource.v2`, the Generator rating probes), adds one entry per moved field group with the cause `makeposseq-runs-again-on-a-reduced-circuit`, and the pin `a_second_makeposseq_changes_nothing` holds the engine's values. `modes:makeposseq/makeposseq_pc.dss` converts once and moves nothing. The rows of `props_r4133_pins.rs` and `props_r4133_replay.rs` that read the twice-converted deck (`load_kva_after_makeposseq_is_the_exact_typed_conversion`, `load_kw_kvar_and_xfkva_after_makeposseq_are_the_exact_typed_conversion`, `vsource_isc3_and_puz_after_makeposseq_use_the_full_precision_basekv`, `vsource_mvasc1_and_mvasc3_after_makeposseq_use_the_full_precision_basekv`, the Generator rating rows) read the once-converted pc deck, and the repeat case carries the r4133 values as the divergent ones. `props_r4133_evidence_lock.rs` names the new case. The new entries that scope `powers`, `losses` or deck-wide `voltages` move the two registers of `corpus_gate/ledger.rs` (section 4 conventions). No golden byte moves.

**Upstream report.** Report 100: "MakePosSequence run a second time reduces the circuit again" (Load `kw` divided by 9, Vsource `basekv` by 3 over two runs), measured on r4133 with epri-worker on the repeat deck. Extends report 82, section 6, with the one- and two-phase Fault and the non-passive conductance matrix (a two-phase `gmatrix=[0.12|0 0.12]` fault draws 18660.3 kW after the command against 12440.1 kW before on r4133, as the step-3 row recorded on 2026-10-05, re-measured by the step). Report 101 only as item 8 says.

**Parts.**
- P1: the three splits and the path mentions, byte-faithful, gate by scoped tests, one commit, no behaviour change.
- P2: the pipeline, `PosSeqReduction`, `PosSeqRefusal`, the guard, `pos_seq_refusal` (with the Line's), `ABORTING_SOLUTION`, `is_passive`, `lu_solve`, `inverse`, `SetText`, the five helpers, the refusal and repeat pins. Commit.
- P3: `PosSeqLeg`, `pos_seq_legs` for every class but the two transformer classes, `pos_seq_pattern`, `legs.rs` and its pin, the order pin. Commit.
- P4: the corpus split, the measurement of the repeat case, ledger entries, the two registers of `corpus_gate/ledger.rs`, lock, the population rails and the `TESTING.md` counts, integration re-pins, the topology test. Commit.
- P5: the D16 check, DIVERGENCES entry and the "reads the loss of" sentence of item 9, record, STATUS bullet, report 100 and the report 82 section. Commit.

**Acceptance.**
- Every pin above passes in both lanes. The refusal pins and the repeat pins fail at the step base (a reduced circuit, a second division), the order pin fails at the base (controls in creation order), the helper and leg pins are new code.
- The corpus gate is green with every ledger entry hit, no golden byte moved, the population rails restated with the sentence naming the new case.
- `rg -n "Aborting solution" crates/dss-core/src/exec/make_pos_seq` finds nothing (the suffix is the constant `ABORTING_SOLUTION` of `elements/pd/mod.rs`), `rg -n "make_pos_seq\.rs" crates tests/corpus/props_r4133` finds nothing, and `rg -n "tmp/|\.rs:[0-9]"` over the changed files finds no provenance or line citation.
- The record, the STATUS bullet, the DIVERGENCES entry, report 100 and the report 82 section are in place.

### MPS-02 — Build the node map: phasors, ports and the bus each reduced conductor lands on

<!-- MPS-STEP {"name": "mps02-node-map", "description": "MakePosSequence builds a node map of earth, port and internal nodes with true phasors and writes each bus from it", "lane": "lane-mps02", "tag": "mps02", "state": "mps02", "prior": "makeposseq_matrix_fix", "base": "update", "task": "this section"} -->

**Tier, gate, after.** Executor opus/xhigh, full gate. After MPS-01. Wave 2, beside MPS-07 (disjoint files). It touches the node map, so no other step that touches `exec/make_pos_seq/map.rs` or `ports.rs` runs beside it.

**Files.** `circuit/circuit.rs` (the node-list rule moved into a pure function), `exec/make_pos_seq/map.rs` (new), `exec/make_pos_seq/ports.rs` (new), `exec/make_pos_seq/analyse.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/apply.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/refuse.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/chains.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/sources.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/pc.rs` and `exec/make_pos_seq/controls.rs` (new, each an empty stage), `elements/pos_seq/mod.rs` (`PosSeqMap`, `PosSeqCtx::target`, `PosSeqAction::OpenConductor`), `elements/pos_seq/target/` (new: `mod.rs`, `line.rs`, `lumped.rs`, `source.rs`, `pc.rs`, `winding.rs`, `control.rs`), `elements/pd/transformer/pos_seq.rs` and `elements/pd/auto_trans/pos_seq.rs` (new, the legs), `elements/pd/transformer/mod.rs` and `elements/pd/auto_trans/mod.rs` (the `mod` lines only), `elements/pd/transformer/accessors.rs` and `elements/pd/auto_trans/accessors.rs` (the one-line `pos_seq_legs` of the `impl CktElement` block only), `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/map.rs` (new), records, `investigations/to_opendss/`.

**Scope by item.**
1. `Circuit::process_bus_defs` keeps its behaviour and reads its default-then-explicit node rule from a new pure function `circuit::circuit::terminal_nodes(nphases, nconds, explicit: &[i32]) -> Vec<i32>`. The map calls the same function, so it reads the nodes the solve builds. Note that `reprocess_bus_defs` walks enabled elements only, so the map parses every bus string itself.
2. Topology collection in `map.rs`: for every circuit element in creation order, enabled or disabled, its class kind by the table of section 1.2, its terminals with the node of every conductor, the open state of every conductor (`conductor_closed`), and its legs (`pos_seq_legs`).
3. `pos_seq_legs` for Transformer and AutoTrans, from the `TermRef` pairs their `build_yprim_component` walks: one `Winding` leg per winding and phase with `v_leg` the `Winding::vbase` the stamp uses (with its sign). The AutoTrans returns its series winding as `Through` legs from conductor `p` of its H terminal to conductor `p` of its X terminal, its common winding and a tertiary as `Winding` legs, so the map needs no knowledge of its `set_node_ref` aliasing.
4. The map algorithm of sections 1.2 and 1.3: seeds (with the partly carried Vsource), strong carriers (Line conductors, `Through` legs, the joint transformer solve of section 1.2 step 2 with its reference windings, zero-mean rows per floating set, star rows and the normal equations solved by `elements::pos_seq::project::lu_solve`), the lumped stage in rounds with the terminal-leg rule of section 1.3, repeated rounds, phasor equality to `1e-9`, the consistency checks of section 1.2 step 5. Within a round elements are walked in creation order and nodes in the order the elements reach them, so the node and the two elements a case-1 message names are deterministic.
5. Ports, joins and numbering of section 1.4 in `ports.rs`: the refusal of a Vsource or Isource above three phases (case 2), the walk that closes a group at one balanced set (three nodes 120 degrees apart, or two poles), the balanced-set check of every group as it forms (a lone terminal above three phases, case 3 naming its element alone), the joins in creation order with the balanced-set check of every joined port, and the order check of section 1.4 item 3 there: an element whose `CktElement::pos_seq_pattern` (MPS-01) is `Some(p)` must see `p` on its port up to one common unit factor, phasors equal within `1e-9`, and an element with `None` is accepted in either order.
6. Refusals: cases 1, 2 (the Vsource or Isource above three phases and every element of section 1.4 item 3 off its pattern), 3 (a repeated phasor or a group that is not one balanced set, as it forms or at a join), 4, the lumped conductor between ports of different phasors (D15, stage 2 of section 1.12), the circuit open on some phases (D9), the undetermined winding group (section 1.2 step 2), each with the message of section 1.12.
7. The result `PosSeqMap`, a field `map` of `PosSeqReduction` beside the new `targets: Vec<Option<PosSeqTarget>>` (one slot per element, filled by the stage files of item 11): for each node its value `x`, its phasor and its meaning (earth, port with its reduced node number `j`, internal with `private: bool` and, for a winding star, the solved star value), for each bus its ports, for each element and terminal the reduced node of every conductor, for each element its circuits (pairs of ports with `N_c` and the phasor column) and the `λ` of each leg.
8. Bus write (section 1.11): after the class actions, the applier writes every terminal of every element whose terminals each touch at most one port, from the map, by the rules of section 1.11 (a shunt by the map from its port node to `<port bus>.0`), through `PosSeqAction::SetText` on the class's bus properties (`bus1`, `bus2`, `buses`, `BusH`, `BusNH`, `BusX`, `BusNX`). This replaces `CktElementData::make_pos_sequence_base` for those elements, so the substring rule `is_ground_bus` no longer decides a node. An element with every conductor on node 0 gets `PosSeqAction::Disable`. The applier writes no bus for an element whose plan holds `PosSeqAction::Disable`, whoever put it there: the one-phase transformer off phase 1 of the xfmr deck keeps its buses while its class's present reduction disables it, and reaches the bus write unchanged once MPS-08 keeps it, with no edit of `apply.rs`. An element whose terminal spans two or more ports keeps `make_pos_sequence_base` until MPS-04 retires it. Controls and meters get no bus write (section 1.11).
9. State kept (D9, section 1.11): a new action `PosSeqAction::OpenConductor(terminal, conductor)` the applier replays after an element's writes for each circuit that was open at that terminal. The enabled state is never written except by `Disable`.
10. Nothing else changes in this step: the reduced values stay those of each class's `make_pos_sequence` until MPS-03, MPS-06 and MPS-08.
11. The skeleton later steps fill without touching shared files: `exec/make_pos_seq/analyse.rs` gets its final stage list (element checks, map, eliminate and project, chains and joint stars, sources, power conversion, controls) with one call per stage file (`exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs`, `chains.rs`, `sources.rs`, `pc.rs`, `controls.rs`), each created here filling no slot of `targets`, so the class keeps its present reduction. `elements/pos_seq/target/mod.rs` gets `enum PosSeqTarget { Line(LineTarget), Lumped(LumpedTarget), Source(SourceTarget), Pc(PcTarget), Winding(WindingTarget), Control(ControlTarget) }`, each struct in its own file (`line.rs`, `lumped.rs`, `source.rs`, `pc.rs`, `winding.rs`, `control.rs`) holding the fields sections 1.8 to 1.13 and decision D22 name, each deriving `Default`, and `PosSeqCtx` gets `target: Option<PosSeqTarget>`, which the applier fills from the element's slot. A later step that needs another field adds it to its own struct only, so two lanes of one wave never edit the same file. A stage may fill a slot that a later stage completes (the chains stage puts a carrier's `ν` and factor into its `PcTarget`, the power conversion stage adds the rest).

**Pins** (`exec/tests/make_pos_seq/map.rs`. The map pins call `Dss::analyse_pos_seq` and read `PosSeqMap`, the phasor in degrees in `[0, 360)` to `1e-9`. Every deck starts with `STIFF` unless it says otherwise, every Line not given otherwise is `phases=3 r1=0.1 x1=0.3 length=1`.)

**Divergences.** None expected: every corpus deck keeps its bus spellings (port 1 written bare, earth as `.0`), and the element its class disables keeps its buses. The planner lists the bus of every element of the eight `modes/makeposseq` cases after the command at the step base and after the change and stops if one differs.

**Upstream report.** Report 102: "MakePosSequence renames a bus by its node digits: nodes 4 and above go to earth, node 10 is read as phase 1". The step builds its own deck (the Kersting Example 4.2 pole of R-6 as a 7-conductor Line with line 2 on `c.4.5.6`, its two loads on `c.1.2.3` and `c.4.5.6`), measures it on r4133 with epri-worker and on the pinned dss-python before and after the command, and records the deck and the numbers in the report. A second section of report 102: "an open Line comes back closed after MakePosSequence" (a phase change reallocates the terminals with every conductor closed), measured on r4133 with epri-worker on the deck of `an_open_line_stays_open_after_makeposseq`. No number of another deck goes into it.

**Parts.**
- P1: `terminal_nodes`, topology collection, Line and `Through` propagation, the Vsource seeds (the partly carried source included), case 1 and case 4 on Lines, the first six pins. Commit.
- P2: `pos_seq_legs` for Transformer and AutoTrans, the joint winding solve with its zero-mean and star rows and the wait of a singular group, the transformer pins (the open-delta, three-unit and ungrounded-wye pins included) and the two unit tests. Commit.
- P3: the lumped stage in rounds with the terminal-leg rule, ports with the three-by-three walk, the check of a group as it forms, joins with the balanced-set check, numbering, the order check by pattern, cases 2 (the Vsource and the Isource above three phases, the pattern), 3, D9, D15, the undetermined winding, their pins. Commit.
- P4: the bus write (the shunt spelling and the `Disable` skip), the disable rule, `OpenConductor`, the skeleton of item 11, the bus and state pins. Commit.
- P5: the corpus bus listing, report 102 with both sections measured on the oracles, record, STATUS. Commit.

**Acceptance.** Every pin passes in both lanes. The bus pins (`makeposseq_writes_a_lumped_port_as_node_two`, `the_neutral_reactor_of_a_balanced_wye_bank_is_disabled`, `a_node_above_nine_is_read_by_its_number`, `a_shunt_is_written_from_its_port_to_earth_on_the_port_bus`), `an_open_line_stays_open_after_makeposseq` and every refusal pin fail at the step base, `a_disabled_line_is_reduced_and_stays_disabled` holds a behaviour the step keeps, `a_load_on_a_negative_order_port_is_reduced_on_its_port` may already pass at the base (the planner checks and records it), the map pins are new code. The corpus gate is green with no ledger change and no golden byte moved. `make_pos_sequence_base` is called only for elements whose terminal spans two or more ports. `rg -n "\.invert\(|\.kron\(" crates/dss-core/src/exec/make_pos_seq` finds nothing.

### MPS-03 — Project every one-port element by the map: two forms, internal nodes inside an element, the one- and two-phase instances

<!-- MPS-STEP {"name": "mps03-projection", "description": "MakePosSequence projects Line, Reactor, Capacitor, Fault and GICTransformer by the node map in the impedance or admittance form", "lane": "lane-mps03", "tag": "mps03", "state": "mps03", "prior": "makeposseq_matrix_fix", "base": "update", "task": "this section"} -->

**Tier, gate, after.** Executor opus/xhigh, full gate. After MPS-02 and MPS-07 (both edit the Reactor and Capacitor files) and after queue step 14 (it edits the Line's geometry path). Wave 3, beside MPS-08 (disjoint files), never beside queue steps 14, 17 or 21. Queue steps 2 and 4 are already on `update`.

**Files.** `elements/pos_seq/project.rs`, `elements/pos_seq/mod.rs` (`PosSeqStamp`), `elements/pos_seq/target/line.rs`, `elements/pos_seq/target/lumped.rs`, `elements/traits.rs` (`pos_seq_stamp`), `exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs`, `elements/pd/line/solve.rs`, `elements/pd/line/mod.rs`, `elements/pd/line/tests.rs`, `elements/pd/reactor/solve.rs`, `elements/pd/reactor/tests.rs`, `elements/pd/capacitor/solve.rs`, `elements/pd/capacitor/steps.rs`, `elements/pd/capacitor/tests.rs`, `elements/pd/fault.rs`, `elements/pd/fault/tests.rs`, `elements/pd/gic_transformer/solve.rs`, `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/projection.rs`, `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/limits.rs` (new), `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_pins.rs` and `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_replay.rs` (only the rows the corpus measurement shows moving), `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, `tests/corpus/manifests/population.lock.json`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs` (the two registers, section 4 conventions), records, `investigations/to_opendss/82-makeposseq-matrix-reduction.md`, `investigations/to_opendss/103-*.md`.

**Scope by item.**
1. `elements/pos_seq/project.rs`: `enum Reciprocity { Reciprocal, NonReciprocal }`, `s3(&CMatrix) -> Complex64` (the three-phase closed form with the arithmetic of `pos_seq_self_term` on the symmetric part), `project_z(zb: &CMatrix, w: &CMatrix, r: Reciprocity) -> CMatrix` (`3 G⁻¹ Wᴴ Zb W G⁻¹`) and `project_y(y: &CMatrix, w: &CMatrix, r: Reciprocity) -> CMatrix` (`(1/3) Wᴴ Y W`), with `w` holding one row per conductor (or node) and one column per circuit (or port), entries the phasors. Both dispatch to `s3` when `w` is one column `u·v` with `|u| = 1` and `r` is `Reciprocal` (the matrix read on its symmetric part), and evaluate `(1/3) vᴴ X v` in full for `NonReciprocal` (a Vsource or z1/z2/z0 Reactor stamp, section 1.8). Also `kron(m, keep, elim)`, `strike(m, keep)` and `sym_part(m)`, written on `lu_solve` and `inverse` of MPS-01, and `is_passive` from MPS-01. Unit pins for every instance row of section 1.8.
2. `CktElement::pos_seq_stamp(&self) -> Option<PosSeqStamp>`, default `None`, returning what the element stamps, from the same code its `calc_yprim` uses, without storing anything:
   - Line: `z` total ohm (`z · len / units_convert` for the matrix and symmetrical-component forms) and `c` total farad (`Im(yc)/ω`, `ω = 2π base_frequency`) over its `nphases` conductors, its form (symmetrical components with `r1`, `x1`, `c1`, matrix, geometry, spacing, switch), its length and length units, its ratings. A geometry or spacing Line evaluates its `z` and `c` at the base frequency on a clone of what `make_z_from_geometry` or `make_z_from_spacing` mutates, never from a `z` stored by a solve at another frequency (a harmonic solve), and a geometry the build refuses has been refused by case 6 before this stage runs.
   - Reactor, Capacitor (per step), Fault, GICTransformer: the branch admittance matrix `Yb` per leg (`pos_seq_legs`), the branch impedance `Zb` when the element gives it directly (R + jX, `rmatrix`, `xmatrix`), its spec form (kvar, R + jX, z1/z2/z0, matrix, `cuf`, `cmatrix`, `gmatrix`, `r`, parallel or series) and rated `kvar`/`kV` where given.
3. `exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs`. First, for every lumped element whatever its number of circuits, the form classification of section 1.8 (series, shunt, between phases, mixed) and refusal case 5, so a mixed element of two circuits is refused here as well. Then, per element with at most one circuit (every element whose terminals each touch at most one port):
   1. Read the meaning of each conductor end from the map.
   2. Line: classify the non-phase conductors by section 1.5 and eliminate them in `Z` and `C`. A conductor on an internal node another element touches reads it as earth until MPS-05 lands (the chain).
   3. Lumped: take the form of the classification. Eliminate a private star in the nodal matrix (section 1.5). A star written as `b.4.4.4` has its three conductor rows summed onto the one node before the elimination.
   4. Project by the form: `Z1` by `project_z`, `Y1` by `project_y`, `C1` by `project_y` on `C`, with the three rules of section 1.8 that keep today's three-phase values to the bit (the symmetric-part arithmetic of `s3`, the kept values of an element given in positive-sequence terms, the arithmetic of `pd::line::Line::make_pos_sequence` for a three-phase matrix, geometry or spacing Line).
   5. Fill `LineTarget` (`Z1` and `C1` per unit of the Line's own length, its rating factor `N_c/3`, the kept-value flag) and `LumpedTarget` (`Z1` or `Y1` and its form, per step for a Capacitor, `kvar'`, `kV'` and the projection factor `k` of section 1.13 for a `kvar` element, the Fault's `g1`, the GICTransformer's `k`) and hand them to the class in `PosSeqCtx::target`.
   Lines with more than one phase conductor per node pair (parallel cores) and elements with two or more circuits keep the step-3 reduction until MPS-04 lands.
4. Writes (each class's `make_pos_sequence` turns its target into actions):
   - Line, in the order of section 1.10: `r1`, `x1`, `c1` per unit of its own length (the given values for the symmetrical-component form and for a switch, decision D18), then `phases=1`, then `normamps`, `emergamps` and the seasonal `ratings` times `N_c/3` (a rating not set stays not set), then `units` as today, with the `PrpSequence` clears of today. No switch branch: a switch writes its own constants through the kept-value rule.
   - A delta Reactor or Capacitor writes `conn=wye` first (the `CONN` side effect resets the terminals and conductor count), then `phases=1`, then its values, and the bus write of MPS-02 runs after every class action, so the reduced element has its two terminals.
   - Reactor: `phases=1`. A `kvar` element keeps its form: `kvar'` and `kv'` by the leg rule (section 1.13), `r' = k r`, `rp' = k rp`. A wye R + jX or z1/z2/z0 Reactor of three phases keeps its values (`phases=1` alone, as today). Every other form writes the projected value: series form `r`, `x` with `rp=0` and no curves, parallel form `r=0`, `x = -1/Im(y1)`, `rp = 1/Re(y1)` (decision D19 (6)). A shunt is written from its port node to earth (section 1.11).
   - Capacitor: `phases=1`. A `kvar` bank writes `kvar'` per step and `kv'` by the leg rule. A wye `cuf` bank of equal phases keeps its `cuf`. Any other `cuf` or `cmatrix` bank writes `cuf` per step from that step's projected `C1` (series banks in the impedance form). The series `R` and `XL` of each step are written by section 1.13: times `k` for a wye, one- or two-phase bank, kept (`3/N_c = 1`) for a three-phase delta or `cmatrix` bank.
   - Fault: `phases=1` and `gmatrix=[g1]` when given by `gmatrix` (a passive zero matrix writes `[0]`), `r` kept when given by `r` on three phases.
   - GICTransformer: `phases=1` and its winding resistances (`R1`, `R2`) times `k` (1 for three phases, 3 for one phase to earth).
5. Refusal case 5 (section 1.12).
6. `Reactor::matrix_pos_seq` and the mean-over-all-pairs branches of the Line, Reactor and Capacitor reductions go for every element this step projects. `pos_seq_self_term` with its mean branch stays only for the transitional path of item 3 (parallel cores, two or more circuits) and goes in MPS-04. Its three-phase arithmetic lives on in `s3`. The step-3 pins of the rules this step replaces are rewritten in place, listed under Divergences.

**Pins** (`exec/tests/make_pos_seq/projection.rs` and `limits.rs`, the unit pins in `elements/pos_seq/project.rs`. Every deck starts with `STIFF` unless it says otherwise.)

**Divergences.** `modes:makeposseq/makeposseq_line.dss` on `capi_v0145`: `Line.l_1ph` takes three times its impedance and a third of its capacitance (section 1.8), so its probes and everything downstream of `b6` move. Field-level entries with the cause `makeposseq-one-phase-series-element-kept-per-phase`, held by `a_one_phase_line_takes_three_times_its_impedance_and_a_third_of_its_capacitance`. Every other value of the eight `modes/makeposseq` cases stays the bit it is today by the three rules of section 1.8 (the shunt deck's `rx_mat` and `cap_cmat` by the symmetric-part arithmetic of `s3` on the same matrices, `rx_z`, `rx_z1`, `rx_kvar`, `cap_kvar`, `cap_cuf` and the `r=10` fault by their kept values, the Lines `l_sym`, `l_mi`, `l_sw` by their kept values and `l_mat`, `l_geo`, `l_spc` by the Line arithmetic). The planner measures every cell, and "moves" means beyond the gate floors or the 15-digit rendering a props row compares. It stops if anything but `l_1ph` and its downstream moves, and lists the props readers it checked (`props_r4133_pins.rs::line_b1_and_b0_after_makeposseq_use_the_full_precision_c1`, `reactor_amps_after_makeposseq_are_the_exact_typed_conversion`, `capacitor_cuf_and_amps_after_makeposseq_are_the_exact_typed_conversion`, the `RP39_PINS` list of `props_r4133_replay.rs`, `exec::tests::make_pos_seq::makeposseq_shunt_reproduces_its_three_phase_solution`).

Step-3 pins rewritten in place (no test is deleted), each renamed for the rule of this step on the same matrix with its expected value computed in the test by the formula of section 1.5 or 1.8 on the deck's data: Capacitor `make_pos_sequence_four_conductor_cmatrix_takes_mean_self_minus_mean_mutual`, Reactor `make_pos_sequence_four_conductor_matrix_takes_mean_self_minus_mean_mutual` and `make_pos_sequence_four_conductor_parallel_matrix_takes_mean_self_minus_mean_mutual` (the fourth conductor's node read from the map: private, so Kron-reduced in the element's nodal matrix), `make_pos_sequence_four_conductor_zero_self_term_stays_finite` (its assertion, a finite reduced value and no NaN, kept on the map's reading), Line `make_pos_sequence_four_conductor_line_takes_mean_self_minus_mean_mutual` and `make_pos_sequence_four_wire_geometry_line_takes_mean_self_minus_mean_mutual_per_length` (the neutral by section 1.5, as the P-4W family), Fault `make_pos_sequence_gmatrix_fault_with_no_conductance_draws_nothing` (as `a_zero_gmatrix_fault_reduces_to_no_conductance`), Line `make_pos_sequence_two_phase_line_c1_is_the_shunt_self_term` (its Z1 half becomes `(3/4)(Z11 + Z22 - Z12) = 1.35 + j14.25` for `rmatrix=[1|.2 1]`, `xmatrix=[10|1 10]`, its C1 stays 22/3 nF), the one-phase pins `make_pos_sequence_single_phase_is_base_only`, Reactor `make_pos_sequence_matrix_single_phase_is_empty_edit`, `make_pos_sequence_rx_and_z1_just_set_phases` (below three phases, `3 z`), Capacitor `make_pos_sequence_cmatrix_single_phase_is_base` (`C/3`), and `exec::tests::make_pos_seq::makeposseq_strips_line_node_extension_and_is_idempotent` (the node from the map, the idempotence from the guard of MPS-01).

**Upstream report.** Extends report 82 with sections for the one- and two-phase forms (a one-phase Line kept per phase, the two-phase Line's `(Z11 + Z22)/2 - Z12`, the two-phase delta `kvar` Reactor, the series `R` and `XL` of a one-phase Capacitor kept per phase, a one-phase GICTransformer kept per phase), the impedance form of series elements (the stacked reactor), the neutral retained (the mean over four conductors), the star bank read at zero volts and the phase-to-phase reactor's loss reading. Each section carries the r4133 measurement the step makes with epri-worker. Report 103: "MakePosSequence overwrites the constants of an edited switch Line", measured on r4133 with the pin's deck.

**Parts.**
Eight parts, the ceiling of the step script:
- P1: `elements/pos_seq/project.rs` and its unit pins. Commit.
- P2: `pos_seq_stamp` for the Line (the geometry and spacing clone at the base frequency included), `LineTarget`, the Line eliminations and kept-value rules in `exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs`. Commit.
- P3: `pos_seq_stamp` for Reactor, Capacitor (per step, with its `R` and `XL`), Fault and GICTransformer, `LumpedTarget`, the form classification and case 5 for every lumped element, the private stars. Commit.
- P4: the Line writes in their order, the P-4W family pins, the switch pins. Commit.
- P5: the lateral, the one-phase Line, the rating pins, the limit pins L5 and L6, the Line step-3 pins rewritten. Commit.
- P6: the Reactor, Capacitor, Fault and GICTransformer writes (`conn=wye` first, `R` and `XL` by `k` or kept by `3/N_c`), case 5, their pins, the limit pins L2 and L7, the lumped step-3 pins rewritten. Commit.
- P7: the corpus measurement, ledger, the two registers, lock, props rows, DIVERGENCES entry. Commit.
- P8: the report 82 sections with their r4133 measurements, report 103, record, STATUS. Commit.

**Acceptance.**
- Every pin passes in both lanes. The pins that hold a behaviour the step keeps pass at the step base: `the_stacked_shunt_reactor_takes_the_admittance_form`, `the_two_phase_lateral_keeps_its_charging` (its C1 is the base's), `an_unedited_switch_keeps_the_switch_constants`, `a_series_reactor_feeding_a_delta_load_reduces_exactly`, `a_line_to_line_capacitor_keeps_its_capacitance_to_earth` and `a_delta_capacitor_with_a_series_reactor_keeps_it` if the step-3 rule already gives them (the planner checks), and the unit pins (new code). Every other value pin fails at the step base.
- No capi or r4133 cell moves but `l_1ph` and its downstream (beyond the gate floors and the 15-digit render), and no golden byte moved.
- `rg -n "fn matrix_pos_seq" crates/dss-core/src` finds nothing, the old names of the rewritten step-3 pins listed above no longer occur, and `rg -n "\.invert\(|\.kron\(" crates/dss-core/src/exec/make_pos_seq crates/dss-core/src/elements/pos_seq` finds nothing. The full grep for the mean rule waits for MPS-04.
- The record, the STATUS bullet, the DIVERGENCES entry, the report 82 sections and report 103 are in place.

### MPS-04 — Merge parallel conductors and write coupled elements with phases=M

<!-- MPS-STEP {"name": "mps04-merge-and-coupled", "description": "MakePosSequence merges parallel conductors and writes a multi-circuit element as one coupled element with phases equal to its circuits", "lane": "lane-mps04", "tag": "mps04", "state": "mps04", "prior": "makeposseq_matrix_fix", "base": "update", "task": "this section"} -->

**Tier, gate, after.** Executor opus/xhigh, full gate. After MPS-03 and MPS-08 (the pins with transformers and the load between two phases need the windings and loads of MPS-08). Wave 4, beside MPS-06, never beside queue steps 17 or 21 (the Line files). It touches the map's multi-port write, so no other map step runs beside it.

**Files.** `elements/pos_seq/merge.rs` (new), `elements/pos_seq/project.rs` (the `M > 1` paths and the rewritten `pos_seq_self_term` tests), `elements/pos_seq/mod.rs` (`pos_seq_self_term` and its tests leave), `elements/pos_seq/target/line.rs`, `elements/pos_seq/target/lumped.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/apply.rs`, `elements/pd/line/solve.rs`, `elements/pd/line/tests.rs` (its helper `mean_self_minus_mean_mutual` is rewritten as the formula of the rule its tests now hold), `elements/pd/reactor/solve.rs`, `elements/pd/capacitor/solve.rs`, `elements/pd/fault.rs` (its gmatrix reduction stops calling `pos_seq_self_term` and writes the coupled `gmatrix`), `elements/pd/gic_transformer/solve.rs` (the coupled write), `elements/traits.rs` (the doc of `CktElement::make_pos_sequence` stops linking `make_pos_sequence_base`), `elements/ckt.rs` (`make_pos_sequence_base`, `is_ground_bus` and their two tests leave), `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/coupled.rs` (new), `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/limits.rs`, records, `investigations/to_opendss/82-makeposseq-matrix-reduction.md`.

**Scope by item.**
1. Merge in `merge.rs`: `A` from the conductors that share their (terminal-1 node, terminal-2 node) pair after elimination, `Zb = (Aᵀ Z⁻¹ A)⁻¹`, `Cb = Aᵀ C A`, the core currents `I = Z⁻¹ A Zb i` per merged conductor under `i = W column`, the shares `|I_k|/|i|`, and the bundle rating `rating / max_k share_k` (decision D6: the engine holds one `normamps` and one `emergamps` per Line, a geometry Line taking the first wire's, so every core has the Line's rating), the same factor on `emergamps` and on every entry of the seasonal `ratings`.
2. The `M > 1` projection: `W` with one column per circuit, `Z1 = project_z`, `C1 = project_y` on `C`, the symmetric part written (section 1.10), `A12` returned for the limit pins.
3. Writes with `phases=M`, through `PosSeqAction::SetText`, every number by Rust's shortest round-trip formatting, by the coupled writes of section 1.10: a Line in the order there (`r1`, `x1` at `Z1[1,1]` per unit length, then `phases=M`, then `rmatrix`, `xmatrix`, `cmatrix` per unit length as lower triangles `[m11 | m21 m22 | ...]`, then the ratings, then `units`), a Reactor `phases=M`, `rmatrix`, `xmatrix` (ohm, series or shunt rule), a Capacitor `phases=M`, `cmatrix` (µF, shunt or series rule, `R` and `XL` by `3/N_c`), a Fault `phases=M`, `gmatrix = Re(Y1)`, a GICTransformer `phases=M` and its resistances times `k`. A Capacitor with `R` or `XL` and a GICTransformer whose circuits differ in `N_c` are refused with the message of section 1.12. Ratings per circuit `N_c/3` and the bundle rule, the coupled element writing the smallest over its circuits (stated limit L10).
4. The bus write of section 1.11 for terminals that span several ports (`bus1=b.1.1` for two circuits on one port, a terminal on ports 1 and 2 written bare as the default of `phases=2`), and the transitional paths leave: `CktElementData::make_pos_sequence_base`, `elements::ckt::is_ground_bus` and `pos_seq_self_term`. Their tests are rewritten, not deleted, each under a name that states its new rule on the same input:
   - `elements::ckt::tests::is_ground_bus_quirk_table` as `the_bus_write_reads_every_node_by_its_number` (its spellings as map inputs) and `elements::ckt::tests::make_pos_sequence_base_rename` as `the_bus_write_keeps_a_bare_bus_and_spells_every_other_list` (its spellings through the bus write), both in `coupled.rs`,
   - the six `elements::pos_seq::tests`, moved to `elements::pos_seq::project::tests` on the same matrices: `three_conductors_take_mean_self_minus_mean_mutual` as `s3_of_three_conductors_is_the_positive_sequence_self_term`, `two_conductors_take_the_embedded_self_term` as `s3_of_two_conductors_embedded_in_three_phases`, `four_conductors_take_mean_self_minus_mean_mutual` as `a_fourth_conductor_on_earth_is_kron_reduced_before_the_projection` (the fourth conductor Kron-reduced, then `s3`, section 1.5), `a_non_symmetric_matrix_reduces_like_its_symmetric_part` as `s3_reads_a_reciprocal_matrix_on_its_symmetric_part`, `complex_entries_reduce_part_by_part` as `s3_of_complex_entries`, `one_conductor_is_its_own_term` as `one_conductor_projects_to_three_times_its_impedance` (`3 z`, section 1.8).
   The line-test helper `mean_self_minus_mean_mutual` of `elements/pd/line/tests.rs` is rewritten as the formula of the rule its callers now hold, under a name without the old words.
5. Decision D8: a reduced `phases=2` coupled element is never re-read (the guard of MPS-01). Its pin lives here.

**Pins** (`exec/tests/make_pos_seq/coupled.rs` and `limits.rs`. The Kersting 4.2 decks are the `K42` deck of section 1.16: a 7 x 7 (or 6 x 6) `rmatrix` and `xmatrix` the test builds by R-1 from the data of R-6, written in ohm/mile with `units=mi length=10`, and a `cmatrix` of zeros unless the pin builds it. Loads at 24.94 kV are three-phase wye. `line.dc6` is the six-conductor Line of MPS-02's `a_second_circuit_becomes_port_two`, `bus1=b.1.2.3.1.2.3 bus2=c.1.2.3.4.5.6`, with a `cmatrix` of zeros: its blocks are balanced, so a deck of balanced blocks on it stays in the balanced state and its reduction is exact.)

**Divergences.** None on the corpus (no deck holds a multi-circuit or parallel-core element). The props channel of no deck reads a matrix the step writes. The planner checks both.

**Upstream report.** Extends report 82 with the merge of parallel cores (the mean over nine), the double circuit (the `B.4.5.6` short to earth that report 102 measures) and the coupled element, each measured on r4133 with epri-worker on the pins' decks.

**Parts.**
- P1: `merge.rs`, the shares and the bundle rating, the nine-core and Kersting 6.9 pins, the rating pin. Commit.
- P2: the `M > 1` projection and targets, the Line, Reactor and Capacitor writes in their order through `SetText`, the multi-port bus write. Commit.
- P3: the Fault and GICTransformer coupled writes, the refusal of a coupled element its class cannot hold, the retirement of `make_pos_sequence_base`, `is_ground_bus` and `pos_seq_self_term` with their tests rewritten under the names of item 4. Commit.
- P4: the coupled pins (double circuit, its capacitance, phased alike, Tleis 3.4, duplex, reversed bank, repeat, the two bus-write pins, the coupled shunt Reactor, series Capacitor, Fault and GICTransformer, the Capacitor and GICTransformer refusals). Commit.
- P5: the limit pins L1, L3, L10, report 82, DIVERGENCES entry, record, STATUS. Commit.

**Acceptance.** Every pin passes in both lanes, the merge and coupled pins fail at the step base (a mean over nine and a `c.0` bus at the base), the L3 pin is new (it needed the loads of MPS-08). `rg -n "make_pos_sequence_base|pos_seq_self_term|mean_self_minus_mean_mutual" crates/dss-core/src` finds nothing, and `rg -n "fn is_ground_bus" crates/dss-core/src/elements` finds nothing (the CIM export's own `cim::writer::is_ground_bus` stays). The corpus gate is green, no golden byte moved.

### MPS-05 — Eliminate internal nodes shared by several elements: cable-screen chains and joint stars

<!-- MPS-STEP {"name": "mps05-chains-and-joint-stars", "description": "MakePosSequence eliminates screen chains jointly with each Line taking its section share, and stars of several lumped elements by their nodal matrix", "lane": "lane-mps05", "tag": "mps05", "state": "mps05", "prior": "makeposseq_matrix_fix", "base": "update", "task": "this section"} -->

**Tier, gate, after.** Executor opus/xhigh, full gate. After MPS-04 (and so after MPS-08, whose power conversion target it scales). Wave 5, beside MPS-10 (disjoint files). It is the only step of its wave that changes the elimination every reduction reads.

**Files.** `exec/make_pos_seq/chains.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs` (the shared internal node goes to the chain or the joint star in place of the transitional earth reading of MPS-03), `exec/make_pos_seq/pc.rs` and `elements/pos_seq/target/pc.rs` (the scale of a power conversion carrier), `elements/pos_seq/target/lumped.rs` (the scaled share of a lumped carrier), `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/chains.rs` (new), `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/limits.rs`, records, `investigations/to_opendss/82-makeposseq-matrix-reduction.md`.

**Scope by item.**
1. Chain detection on the map: the connected sets of internal nodes that are not private to one element, with their non-phase Line conductors, lumped branches, the power conversion elements between two of their nodes, and the port-touching lumped or power conversion conductors that end on them (section 1.6).
2. The series solve of section 1.6 (excitation columns, unknowns, equations, the reference row of a chain that reaches earth nowhere in place of that node's current-law row, `X`, `D_ℓ`, `Z1_ℓ`). Columns follow the series continuity rule. The linear system is assembled densely per chain and solved by `elements::pos_seq::project::lu_solve`, one right-hand side per column.
3. The shunt rule of section 1.6: struck when the chain reaches earth, one equipotential body with zero total charge when it does not.
4. The joint star of section 1.6: nodal assembly (a power conversion element by the nominal admittance its `PosSeqLeg::s_leg` and `v_leg` give, `y = conj(s_leg) / v_leg²` per leg), Kron, `Y1`, the own shares `σ_e`, the class-aware scale of item 4 there with its refusal, the port-less elements disabled. The interface with the power conversion stage: `chains.rs` puts, per carrier, its star voltages `ν` and its factor (`f_pc`, or the real `f`) into the carrier's slot of `PosSeqReduction::targets` as a `PcTarget` with only those fields set (two new fields of `elements/pos_seq/target/pc.rs`, this step's file), and `exec/make_pos_seq/pc.rs` completes that target when it applies the leg rule of section 1.13 (`Δw` with `ν`, `S'` with `conj(f)`), so the written `kw`, `kvar` and `kv` follow section 1.13 with the factor. A lumped carrier gets its scaled share in its `LumpedTarget`: a `cuf` Capacitor its `cuf` per step, a `kvar` Capacitor or Reactor its `kvar'` times `f` with `r'` and `rp'` divided by `f`, an R + jX Reactor `r`, `x` from its admittance (section 1.6 item 5). `analyse.rs` is not edited.
5. A power conversion or lumped conductor between a port node and a chain node: zero volts, open in the solve (decision D14, stated limit L9).
6. The degenerate cases reproduce section 1.5 to T1 on the P-4W family of MPS-03, which stays green unchanged.
7. A winding star (section 1.6, the paragraph after the joint star): an internal node that a transformer winding's star reaches is read by every other element at the star value of the map's transformer solve, the elements whose conductors all end there or on earth land on node 0 and are disabled, and the node goes to no chain and no joint star.

**Pins** (`exec/tests/make_pos_seq/chains.rs` and `limits.rs`. The Tleis 3.5 decks are the `T35` deck of section 1.16, source on `b`, and its one-Line form `new line.s1 phases=6 bus1=b.1.2.3.0.0.0 bus2=e.1.2.3.0.0.0 length=1 units=km` with the same matrices.)

Disjointness with MPS-04: MPS-02 left the stage list in `analyse.rs`, the empty `sources.rs` and the source target in its own file, so this step edits neither `analyse.rs` nor any file MPS-04 edits.

**Scope by item.**
1. `sources.rs`: for each Vsource, Isource, GICLine and GICsource, the `w` of its port from the map, `E` or `I` per conductor as the element stamps it (`pc/vsource/source.rs`, the Isource and GIC equivalents, sequence included), `E1 = (1/N) wᴴ E` or `I1 = (1/3) Wᴴ I`, and for a Vsource and a GICLine `Z1 = project_z(Zs, w, Reciprocity::NonReciprocal)` on the primitive matrix it stamps (`Vsource::z`, a stamp with `Z2 ≠ Z1` is non-reciprocal by construction, and a GICLine's with `Reciprocity::Reciprocal`), with the kept values of section 1.8 for a three-phase Vsource.
2. Writes: Vsource `phases=1`, `basekv = |E1|/1000/pu` (kV), `angle = arg(E1)` (degrees), `r1`, `x1` (decision D19). Isource `phases=1`, `amps = |I1|`, `angle = arg(I1)`. GICLine `phases=1`, `volts = |E1|` (0 for its zero-sequence EMF), `angle`, `r`, `x` from `Z1`. GICsource `phases=1`, `volts=0`.
3. A two-phase Vsource stands its poles 180 degrees apart in the engine, so its port is `(1, -1)` and the formula gives the pole voltage `kV/2` behind `1.5 (Zs - Zm)` (section 1.9). A one-phase Vsource keeps its kV and takes `3 Zs` (section 1.9). A Vsource reached through a winding takes `angle - θ` with `θ = arg w` of its first node (section 1.9).

### MPS-07 — Delta Capacitor and Reactor: the conductor count in either order, the delta Reactor matrix of legs, the nodal delta cmatrix, a Reactor given one matrix

<!-- MPS-STEP {"name": "mps07-delta-elements", "description": "A delta Capacitor or Reactor has N plus 1 conductors below three phases in either property order, a delta Reactor matrix holds leg quantities and a delta cmatrix is nodal", "lane": "lane-mps07", "tag": "mps07", "state": "mps07", "prior": "makeposseq_matrix_fix", "base": "update", "task": "this section"} -->

**Tier, gate, after.** Executor opus/xhigh, full gate. After MPS-01. Wave 2, beside MPS-02 (disjoint files).

**Files.** `elements/pd/capacitor/accessors.rs`, `elements/pd/capacitor/solve.rs`, `elements/pd/capacitor/steps.rs`, `elements/pd/capacitor/tests.rs`, `elements/pd/reactor/accessors.rs`, `elements/pd/reactor/solve.rs`, `elements/pd/reactor/mod.rs`, `elements/pd/reactor/tests.rs`, `support/cmatrix/mod.rs` (a ring stamp of a leg matrix beside `CMatrix::stamp_delta_series`, if the planner places it there), `solution/ymatrix.rs` (its tests' refused fixture only), `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, `tests/corpus/manifests/population.lock.json`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs` (the two registers), the corpus decks only if a scan finds a fixture to add (and then the population rails of the section 4 conventions: `corpus_gate/scratch.rs`, `corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`, `props_r4133_evidence_lock.rs`, `TESTING.md`), records, `investigations/to_opendss/82-makeposseq-matrix-reduction.md`, `investigations/to_opendss/NOT-APPLICABLE-TO-R4133.md`.

**Scope by item.**
1. Corpus scan first, by the engine's own parser so that continuation lines (`~`), `edit` lines, `like=` copies and abbreviated or positional property names are read as the solve reads them: a throwaway probe test (not committed) compiles every deck of `tests/corpus` and of `.inputs/electricdss-tst` on the step's base, walks every Capacitor and Reactor with `conn=delta`, and lists each whose conductor count differs from the value of item 2 (`N + 1` for `N = 1, 2`, `N` for `N >= 3`), with the deck. A deck that does not compile is listed apart with its first error. The list goes into the step's state folder and its count into the record.
2. Conductor count (section 1.14): in the `CONN` and `PHASES` arms of the property side effects of `pd/capacitor/accessors.rs` and `pd/reactor/accessors.rs`, a delta element has `N + 1` conductors for `N = 1, 2` and `N` for `N >= 3`, whatever the order of `conn=` and `phases=`. The `PHASES` arm with an unchanged phase count no longer forces `nphases + 1`.
3. Delta Reactor matrix (section 1.14): `rmatrix`/`xmatrix` of a delta Reactor hold the leg impedance matrix `Zb`, legs in the ring order of `stamp_delta_series` (leg `i` between conductor `i` and `i + 1`, the last to conductor 1 for `N >= 3`, to the extra conductor for `N = 1, 2`). The stamp is `Y_nodal = Bᵀ Zb⁻¹ B` on terminal 1 with `B` the ring incidence. `Reactor::refusal` drops the `Matrices` half of its delta arm and keeps the `SymComponents` half: a delta z1/z2/z0 Reactor of two or more phases stays refused with its present message, since no rule of this plan defines its stamp. For `Parallel=yes` the leg admittances are `R⁻¹` and `-X⁻¹` on the ring.
4. Delta Capacitor `cmatrix` (decision D4): for `N >= 3` the nodal matrix on terminal 1, the same block the wye twin with a grounded `bus2` stamps. `Capacitor::refusal` refuses a delta `cmatrix` for `N = 1, 2` only, with its present message.
5. A Reactor given one of `rmatrix`, `xmatrix` (section 1.14): the other is a zero matrix, no message. Series form `Zb = R + jX` with the missing part zero, a singular `Zb` stays the singular-matrix refusal of step 3. Parallel form: a missing part is no branch (no `R⁻¹` or no `-X⁻¹` term), and the edit raises no error. A given parallel-form matrix is checked for passivity first (`elements::pos_seq::project::is_passive` on its symmetric part): a matrix with a negative eigenvalue (an invertible matrix with a zero diagonal entry is one) is refused with `Reactor.p: rmatrix is not passive, so the parallel reactor draws power it cannot hold. Specify a passive rmatrix. Aborting solution.` (decision D19 (4)), before the inversion that called it singular.
6. Reduction under MakePosSequence until MPS-03 lands: a delta matrix Reactor and a delta `cmatrix` bank reduce by the admittance form of their nodal stamp on phases `1..N` read as `(1, a², a)` (the step-3 helper `pos_seq_self_term` on the nodal matrix, `N <= 3`). MPS-03 replaces it with the map's `W` and keeps the values.
7. Fixtures that relied on the refusals move: `solution::ymatrix::tests::a_refused_build_leaves_no_y_and_logs_no_build_event`, `a_refused_build_keeps_each_bus_voltage_across_a_renumbering` and Capacitor `a_disabled_delta_cmatrix_bank_leaves_the_solve_to_the_rest` use a two-phase delta `cmatrix` bank, Reactor `a_refused_reactor_set_aside_by_disable_leaves_the_solve_to_the_rest` a matrix of another order.

**Pins** (in the class `tests.rs`, every deck on `STIFF`).

| pin | deck | expected | tol. | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `a_delta_element_has_three_conductors_in_either_order` (Capacitor and Reactor) | `conn=delta phases=3` and `phases=3 conn=delta`, and both orders for `phases=1` and `phases=2` | `nconds` 3, 3, 2, 2, 3, 3 | exact | item 2, section 1.14 |

(3.138c), Clarke Vol. I p. 451 |

Specify a passive rmatrix. Aborting solution.` and 482 | exact | decision D19 (4) |

**Divergences.** The decks the scan lists with `conn=delta` before `phases=3`: on `capi_v0145` (four conductors there) their cells move, held by the conductor-count pin, entries with the cause `delta-conn-before-phases-gives-four-conductors`. On `r4133` (three conductors) nothing moves. Every deck holding a delta `cmatrix` bank: both oracles solve it differently (they stamp a delta `cmatrix` as an active element delivering 67.7 kW), entries with the cause `delta-cmatrix-stamped-as-an-active-element`, held by `a_delta_cmatrix_bank_is_the_nodal_matrix`. Every deck holding a delta matrix Reactor: the step measures what each oracle stamps for it and adds the moved field groups with the cause `delta-matrix-reactor-stamped-by-its-legs`, held by `a_delta_matrix_reactor_stamps_its_legs`. The new entries that scope `powers`, `losses` or deck-wide `voltages` move the two registers of `corpus_gate/ledger.rs`. Step-3 pins rewritten in place: Capacitor `a_delta_cmatrix_bank_refuses_the_solve` (as `a_one_or_two_phase_delta_cmatrix_stays_refused` for two phases and `a_delta_cmatrix_bank_is_the_nodal_matrix` for three), Reactor `a_delta_matrix_reactor_refuses_the_solve` (as `a_delta_matrix_reactor_stamps_its_legs`), `a_matrix_reactor_without_both_matrices_refuses_the_solve` (as `a_reactor_given_xmatrix_only_has_no_resistance`), and `make_pos_sequence_delta_reactor_keeps_its_power` (its four-conductor guard half).

**Upstream report.** Extends report 82, section 7, with the fix and the r4133 re-measurement (epri-worker) of the definitions with no stamp: the delta `cmatrix` stamped as an active element (67.7 kW), the matrix Reactor given one matrix (access violation on both engines) and the delta matrix Reactor (what each oracle stamps for its leg matrix, measured on the deck of `a_delta_matrix_reactor_stamps_its_legs`). The conductor count is a dss_capi fault r4133 does not have (three conductors on r4133 in either order, re-measured by the step), so it gets no report and an entry in `investigations/to_opendss/NOT-APPLICABLE-TO-R4133.md` with the measurement.

**Parts.**
- P1: the corpus scan, the conductor count in both classes, its pins. Commit.
- P2: the delta Reactor leg matrix, the delta `cmatrix`, the refusals narrowed, the passivity check, their pins, the reduction of item 6. Commit.
- P3: the Reactor given one matrix of item 5, the moved fixtures, the corpus measurement, ledger, lock, DIVERGENCES entry, record, STATUS, the report 82 section and the NOT-APPLICABLE entry. Commit.

**Acceptance.** Every pin passes in both lanes. The count, stamp, one-matrix and passivity pins fail at the step base, `a_one_or_two_phase_delta_cmatrix_stays_refused` and `a_delta_z1_reactor_stays_refused` hold behaviour the step keeps. The corpus gate is green with every ledger entry hit. No golden byte moved unless the scan finds a golden deck with the property order, which the step then lists with its cause.

### MPS-08 — Rated power and rated voltage at every phase count: Load, Generator, PVSystem, Storage, WindGen, Transformer, AutoTrans and the other power conversion classes

<!-- MPS-STEP {"name": "mps08-power-conversion", "description": "MakePosSequence writes the rated power and voltage of every power conversion element and winding by the leg rule for one two and three phases, and keeps a one phase transformer", "lane": "lane-mps08", "tag": "mps08", "state": "mps08", "prior": "makeposseq_matrix_fix", "base": "update", "task": "this section"} -->

**Tier, gate, after.** Executor opus/xhigh, full gate. After MPS-02 (it reads the map's `λ`, ports and legs). Wave 3, beside MPS-03 (disjoint files).

**Files.** `exec/make_pos_seq/pc.rs`, `elements/pos_seq/target/pc.rs`, `elements/pos_seq/target/winding.rs`, `elements/pc/load/accessors.rs`, `elements/pc/generator/accessors.rs`, `elements/pc/pvsystem/accessors.rs`, `elements/pc/storage/accessors.rs`, `elements/pc/windgen/accessors.rs`, `elements/pc/vccs/accessors.rs`, `elements/pc/ind_mach012/accessors.rs`, `elements/pc/upfc/accessors.rs`, `elements/pc/vs_converter/accessors.rs` (its inline `mod tests`, the class has no `tests.rs`), the `tests.rs` of the other classes (`load`, `generator`, `pvsystem`, `storage`, `ind_mach012`, `vccs`, `upfc`, `windgen`), `elements/pd/transformer/accessors.rs`, `elements/pd/transformer/tests.rs`, `elements/pd/auto_trans/accessors.rs`, `elements/pd/auto_trans/tests.rs`, `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/pc.rs` (every pin of the table below), `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, `tests/corpus/manifests/population.lock.json`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs` (the two registers), `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_pins.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_replay.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs` (the `t1_off` assertions), `crates/dss-test-harness/src/harness/mod.rs` (`CAPI_NODEREF_ADDS`, `CAPI_NODEREF_DROPS`), `TESTING.md` (the at-bus witness), `crates/dss-core/tests/golden_reports.rs` (the AutoTrans loading if it moves), and, if item 4 adds the witness case, `tests/corpus/modes/makeposseq/makeposseq_xfmr_disabled.dss`, `tests/corpus/modes/manifest.json` and the population rails of the section 4 conventions, records, `investigations/to_opendss/104-*.md`, `105-*.md`, `106-*.md`, `109-*.md`.

**Scope by item.**
1. `exec/make_pos_seq/pc.rs`: for each power conversion element and each transformer, from the map and the legs: the port of each terminal, `λ` and `Δw` of each leg, `V_leg`, `S_leg`, then `kV'` (on the leg of largest `|Δw_k| / V_leg,k`) and `S'` by the leg rule of section 1.13 with `f = 1` (MPS-05 adds the joint-star factor), and the factor 1/3 on every other rated quantity. A private star (one node that only this element's legs reach) is eliminated inline here as a scalar: `ν = Σ_k y_k w(plus_k) / Σ_k y_k` with `y_k = conj(s_leg,k)/v_leg,k²` (decision D14, section 1.5), with no call into `elements/pos_seq/project.rs`, which MPS-03 edits in the same wave. The result goes into the element's `PcTarget` or `WindingTarget` slot.
2. Writes, `phases=1`, `conn=wye`, `kv'`, the power `S'` in the form the class was given (Load `kw` and `kvar` as today, Generator and WindGen `kw` and `pf`, Storage and PVSystem their rated powers), and every other rated quantity divided by 3, for one, two and three phases alike:
   - Load: `kw`, `kvar`, `kva`, `xfkva`, `kWh` (`pf`, `%mean`, `%stddev`, `kWhDays` stay).
   - Generator: `kw` (with `pf`), `kva`, `mva`, `minkvar`, `maxkvar`. `FuelkWh` stays (section 1.13: the fuel check burns the balanced-total power the registers sample).
   - WindGen: `kw` (with `pf`), `kvar` when given, `kva`, `mva`. `Pss`, `Qss` (per unit of the turbine), `N_WTG`, `Vss`, `Rthev`, `Xthev` and the turbine data feed the dynamic model, which is out of scope, and stay.
   - PVSystem: `kva`, `pmpp`, `kvar`, `kvarmax`, `kvarmaxabs` (percentages, `pf` and `irradiance` stay).
   - Storage: `kwrated`, `kva`, `kwhrated`, `kwhstored`, `kw`, `kvar`, `kvarmax`, `kvarmaxabs` (percentages stay).
   - IndMach012, VCCS, UPFC, VSConverter: the lists of decision D10.
   - Transformer and AutoTrans: section 1.13. A one-phase transformer on any phase is kept (the `Disable` of today goes), and the map writes its buses (MPS-02 item 8 then reaches it). A bank of one-phase units between one pair of ports (the one-phase transformers whose two terminals lie on the same pair of ports, read from the map, with every one-phase Line conductor and lumped branch between the same two ports) is projected as one set in `exec/make_pos_seq/pc.rs`: the set's solve with `elements::pos_seq::project::lu_solve` (a positive-sequence current imposed at the far port, the units as ideal transformers with ratio, tap and series impedance, the conductors as impedances, the current law at the far port's nodes), `Z_set`, each unit's `kVA'`, `%R`, `%X` and `α_I` into its `WindingTarget` (two new fields of `elements/pos_seq/target/winding.rs`, this step's file, `alpha_i` read by MPS-10 and filled for every winding by the rule of decision D22: 1 for a three-phase wye or delta winding, `2/3` for a two-phase wye winding, `|Δw_r| / 3` for a one-phase winding alone, the set's share for a unit of a bank), and `PosSeqAction::Disable` into the slot of a conductor the solve leaves without current (the Line's slot that MPS-03's projection filled is replaced, as the stage order of section 1.12 allows, and the applier then writes no bus for it, MPS-02 item 8). A balanced bank comes out as the bullet before it (`kVA/3`, `%R`, `%X` kept), an open-delta pair as `kVA/(2√3)` with `%R`, `%X` times `2/√3` and its jumper disabled.
3. Each written property goes through the typed setter or `SetText`, in one edit.
4. The witness of the at-bus classes B and C. `makeposseq_xfmr.dss` carries both today through the disabled `t1_off` (capi's fast path reads its stale node references at `b4`, and the class-B loss is at `b1`). Once the port keeps `t1_off`, the capi replay over the port's state no longer matches the oracle on that deck: the step measures the at-bus field group of the deck on `capi_v0145`, excludes what moves with the cause `makeposseq-disables-a-one-phase-transformer-off-phase-one`, and re-measures `CAPI_NODEREF_ADDS` and `CAPI_NODEREF_DROPS` (exact, asserted by `check_at_bus_populations`), restated with a sentence. To keep a witness, the step builds `modes/makeposseq/makeposseq_xfmr_disabled.dss`, the xfmr deck with `t1_off` disabled by `edit transformer.t1_off enabled=no` after the first solve and before `makeposseq`, so the port and the oracle both hold a disabled element with stale references. If its capi at-bus lists carry classes B and C, it becomes a corpus case (`engines` as the xfmr deck, its lock line, the population rails of the section 4 conventions) and `TESTING.md` names it as the witness. If they do not, the case is not added, and the record and `TESTING.md` state that the witness of classes B and C is gone and by how much the two constants dropped.

**Divergences.** On `capi_v0145`: `makeposseq_xfmr.dss` (`Transformer.t1_ok` kVA by 3 against by 1, `Transformer.t1_off` kept against disabled with its buses `b1` and `b6`, and everything downstream of `b6`), `makeposseq_pc.dss` and its repeat case (`PVSystem.pv1` `kva`, `pmpp`, `Storage.st1` `kva`, `kwhrated`, `kwhstored`), `makeposseq_ctrl.dss` (`Storage.st1`). Each moved field group gets an entry with its cause (`makeposseq-divides-by-the-phase-count`, `makeposseq-disables-a-one-phase-transformer-off-phase-one`, `makeposseq-leaves-pv-and-storage-ratings`) and the pin above that holds it. `corpus_gate.rs` (`the_makeposseq_xfmr_deck_reports_the_at_bus_lists_the_port_computes`, `the_makeposseq_xfmr_at_bus_wires_are_each_channels_own_walk`, `makeposseq_xfmr_solved`) asserts `t1_off` kept with its buses, and the rail `the_g1_4d_pins_the_docs_cite_exist_exactly_once` keeps naming them. The props rows of `props_r4133_pins.rs` for Generator, Transformer and AutoTrans are re-pinned where they move. A golden byte moves only where `golden_reports.rs` reads the xfmr deck's loading after the command: the step lists it. The at-bus field group of the xfmr deck and the two harness constants move by item 4. The new entries that scope `powers`, `losses` or deck-wide `voltages` move the two registers of `corpus_gate/ledger.rs`.

Pins whose rule this step replaces, rewritten in place under a name that states the new rule (section 0), each listed with its old and new name in the record: `elements::pc::generator::tests::makeposseq_generator_single_phase` (one phase takes a third), `elements::pd::transformer::tests::make_pos_sequence_1ph_off_phase1_disables` and `elements::pd::auto_trans::tests::make_pos_sequence_1ph_off_phase1_disables` (kept with a third of its kVA), `make_pos_sequence_1ph_on_phase1_survives` of both classes (a third of its kVA), `exec::tests::make_pos_seq::pc::makeposseq_converts_transformer_and_disables_off_phase1` (kept), `elements::pc::ind_mach012::tests::makeposseq_indmach012_is_no_base_and_empty` and `elements::pc::upfc::tests::makeposseq_upfc_is_no_base_and_empty` (reduced by decision D10), `elements::pc::vccs::tests::makeposseq_vccs_single_phase_is_base_only` and `makeposseq_vccs_multiphase_sets_phases_1` (`prated` by 3), `elements::pc::vs_converter::accessors::tests::makeposseq_vsconverter_non2_forces_phases2_ndc1` and `makeposseq_vsconverter_2phase_is_base_only` (the D10 ratings), `elements::pc::pvsystem::tests::makeposseq_pv_single_phase` and `elements::pc::storage::tests::makeposseq_storage_single_phase` (every rated power by 3). A test among them whose assertion still holds under the new rule keeps its name and the record says so.

**Upstream report.** Report 104: "MakePosSequence divides rated power by the phase count, not by three" (a two-phase machine by 2, a one-phase machine and a one-phase transformer not at all), report 105: "MakePosSequence leaves the PVSystem and Storage energy and kvar ratings", report 106: "MakePosSequence disables a one-phase transformer that is not on phase 1" with a second section "an open-delta regulator bank with its jumper is reduced to a kept unit shorted by the jumper" (measured on the deck of `an_open_delta_regulator_pair_with_its_jumper_is_projected_as_one_set`: the unit on phases 3 and 2 disabled, the jumper kept at its 1e-3 ohm across the kept unit), report 109: "MakePosSequence leaves an IndMach012 and a UPFC multi-phase and keeps the rated power of a VCCS and a VSConverter", each measured on r4133 with epri-worker.

**Parts.**

- P2: PVSystem, Storage, IndMach012, VCCS, UPFC, VSConverter, their pins, the replaced class pins rewritten. Commit.

The corpus gate is green with every ledger entry hit, the at-bus populations and the two harness constants restated, and the witness of classes B and C kept or its loss recorded. 1. The feeder deck, every part of the procedure in one circuit, every load `model=2` (the matrices of `p4w`, `sn` and `lat` are those of the P-4W and lateral decks of section 1.16, those of `dc` the 7 x 7 the test builds by R-1 from R-6 phased alike, with a `cmatrix` of zeros):

2. Its pin `the_whole_procedure_keeps_the_power_of_a_mixed_feeder`:
   - `dS/S` below `1e-2` (T6, the argument of the bound: the parts carry 6.4e-5 (P-4W), 3.4e-4 (the lateral's loads to earth), 6.3e-5 (the shared neutral), a nearly reciprocal double circuit, exact stars, sources and power conversion elements, and the one-phase branch behind `t1`, whose load is 50 kW of about 3.4 MW. `v2` stands at `angle=-30`, the phasor the map gives `g.1` behind the default lagging `sub` bank, so it floats with its node, and its 1 + j5 ohm limit what it exchanges with the feeder to the drop of `g.1` below 0.24 kV: at most about 25 V over about 5.1 ohm, some 5 A and 1.2 kVA, 4e-4 of the feeder's power, of which its L2 error is a fraction), the measured value recorded,

   - every reduced element's written values equal to the formulas of section 1 evaluated in the test on the deck's data (T1): `line.p4w` (Kron in `Z`, struck in `C`), `line.dc` (`phases=2`, its 2 x 2 matrices), `line.lat` (`(3/4)(Z11 + Z22 - Z12)`), the capacitor star with its neutral reactor (joint star, `rn` disabled), `line.sn` (struck neutral) with its loads to earth, `transformer.t1` kept with a third of its kVA, `vsource.v2` with its kV kept, `angle 0` (its `-30` less the map's `θ = -30`) and three times its impedance (`r1 3`, `x1 15`), `pvsystem.pv` and `storage.st` with a third of their ratings,
   - no multi-phase element left (section 1.12): every circuit element of the reduced circuit, enabled or disabled, has `phases` equal to its number of circuits (1 for every element on a single port, 2 for `line.dc`).
3. The MakePosSequence entries of `DIVERGENCES.md` that the plan's steps added are joined into one entry that states the procedure as it stands, keeping every exclusion and pin name. Report 82 gets its section list checked against the steps' additions.

**Acceptance.** The feeder pin passes in both lanes. The written values it asserts differ from those the engine of the MPS-01 base writes on the same deck (the mean over four conductors on `p4w`, the second circuit of `dc` renamed to earth, `t1` on phase 1 kept with its kVA undivided, `pv` and `st` with their `kva`, `pmpp` and energy ratings undivided), read with a probe on that tree whose output goes into the record (the pin itself uses helpers that tree does not have). Every pin named in section 7 exists by name: for each name, its last `::` segment `<fn>` is found by `rg -n "fn <fn>\b" crates/dss-core/src`, and the list goes into the record.

### MPS-10 — Rescale each control setting by what the reduction does to its reading, dispatch power at the balanced total, and read no residual on a positive-sequence circuit

<!-- MPS-STEP {"name": "mps10-controls", "description": "MakePosSequence rescales each control voltage and current setting by the factor the reduction applies to its reading, a dispatcher moves power at the balanced total it reads, and a control on a positive-sequence circuit reads no residual", "lane": "lane-mps10", "tag": "mps10", "state": "mps10", "prior": "makeposseq_matrix_fix", "base": "update", "task": "this section"} -->

**Tier, gate, after.** Executor opus/xhigh, full gate. After MPS-04 (the circuits of a coupled target) and MPS-08 (the reduced windings and power conversion elements). Wave 5, beside MPS-05 (disjoint files).

**Files.** `exec/make_pos_seq/controls.rs`, `elements/pos_seq/target/control.rs`, the `accessors.rs` (where its `make_pos_sequence` lives) of `elements/control/reg_control`, `cap_control`, `relay`, `recloser`, `storage_controller`, `gen_dispatcher`, `espvl_control`, `upfc_control`, `inv_control`, `exp_control`, `swt_control` and of `elements/pd/fuse`, the meters under `elements/meter` only where the audit of item 1 finds a setting, the compute files of `relay`, `recloser` and `fuse` where they form a residual or a negative-sequence quantity (item 4), `elements/control/gen_dispatcher/mod.rs` and `compute.rs` and `elements/control/storage_controller/mod.rs` and `compute.rs` (the dispatch of item 6), `solution/controls/dispatch.rs` (the StorageController reader of item 5, the `power_scale` of the dispatch environments), the `tests.rs` of those classes, `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/controls.rs` (new, every pin of this step), `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, `tests/corpus/manifests/population.lock.json`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs` (the two registers), `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_pins.rs` (only the control rows the measurement shows moving), records, `investigations/to_opendss/110-*.md`.

**Scope by item.**
1. Class audit first. For every control and meter class, the list of each setting that the class compares with a quantity it reads through its monitored or controlled element, with that quantity (a voltage to earth, a voltage between two phases, a voltage across a winding, a current, a power, a residual, a negative-sequence quantity), the code that reads it and, for a power, whether the reader gives the balanced total on a positive-sequence circuit. The list goes into the step's state folder and its table into the record. Decision D22 names the minimum it must cover. The list also holds every phase selector of a control (CapControl `PTPhase`, `CTPhase`, RegControl `PTphase`, any other the audit finds) and every comparison of a reading with the controlled or monitored element's own rating or present power (the CapControl `Pf` guards against `TotalKvar`, any other the audit finds). Rescaled: RegControl `PTRatio`, `CTPrim` and `RemotePTRatio` (its `vreg`, `band`, `R`, `X` and their reverse twins stay on the 120 V base), CapControl `PTRatio` and `CTRatio`, Relay and Recloser phase pickups in amps (`PhaseTrip`, `PhaseInst` and the fast and slow pickups the class holds) and the distance reach in ohm, Fuse `RatedCurrent`, and the StorageController `kWTarget` and `kWTargetLow` of its current modes (kA, `α_I`). Kept, each read as a balanced-total power through `terminal_power` (`α_S = 1`): the CapControl `OnSetting` and `OffSetting` of a `Type` that compares a kW, kvar or kVA, the `PhaseInst` of a Relay of `type=revpower` (kW), the StorageController `kWTarget`, `kWTargetLow`, `kWBand`, `kWBandLow`, `kWThreshold` and seasonal targets of its power modes, the GenDispatcher `kWLimit`, `kWBand`, `kvarLimit`, and the ESPVLControl `kWBand`, `kvarLimit`. A setting in per unit of the target's own base (InvControl, ExpControl), a time, a count, a curve and a multiplier of a curve stay. A meter reports and acts on nothing, so its readings stay as the reduced circuit gives them.
2. `controls.rs`: for each control and meter in creation order, from the map, the factors of decision D22 for the terminal and conductors it reads: `α_V` (1 for a conductor of a port to earth, `1/|w_p - w_q|` between two phase conductors of one port, `1/|Δw_r|` across a winding) and `α_I` (`N_c/3` for a circuit of `N_c` phases, and for a winding its reduced terminal current over its real terminal current: 1 for a three-phase wye or delta winding, `2/3` for a two-phase wye winding, `|Δw_r| / 3` for a one-phase winding alone, decision D22). They go into the control's `ControlTarget { alpha_v, alpha_i }` slot. For a winding, `α_I` is read from the target's `WindingTarget::alpha_i` (MPS-08), which holds that factor, and the set's share for a unit of a bank between one pair of ports (`1/2` for an equal open-delta pair). A power has no factor (`α_S = 1`). A target whose circuits differ in factor gives the first circuit's (stated limit L13).
3. Each control's `make_pos_sequence` writes every audited voltage and current setting times its factor (a ratio and a setting in volts or amps by the factor of what they meet, a setting in ohm compared with a voltage over a current by `α_V/α_I`), through the typed setters or `SetText`, in one edit, after its target (decision D16). A phase selector above the reduced target's conductor count is written as 1 through the same edit path, so the `PTPHASE` and `CTPHASE` clamps of the CapControl and the `recalc` of the RegControl run on it. A power setting is not written.
4. A control on a circuit whose `Circuit::positive_sequence` is set reads a residual (the sum of its terminal's phase currents or voltages) and a negative-sequence quantity as zero: the ground elements of Relay and Recloser and the 46 and 47 Relay types never act there. This holds for a circuit the user declares positive-sequence too (decision D8), since the model holds no zero or negative sequence.
5. The StorageController reader (decision D22, `α_S = 1`): `solution::controls::dispatch::StorageDispEnv::control_power` applies the positive-sequence factor 3 once. Its one-phase branch takes `CktElement::terminal_power`, which already applies it, so the trailing multiplication by 3 applies to the multi-phase branch only (the per-conductor sum, which carries no factor of its own). The code comment that calls the double factor upstream behaviour goes, and the doc of the method states the rule and names the pin. Upstream bug, fixed and pinned, report 110.
6. Dispatch at the balanced total (decision D22): a trait method `power_scale(&self) -> f64` with the default 1 on `elements::control::gen_dispatcher::GenDispatchEnv` and `elements::control::storage_controller::StorageDispatchEnv`, implemented in `solution/controls/dispatch.rs` for `GenDispEnv` and `StorageDispEnv` as 3 when `sys.positive_sequence` is set and 1 otherwise. With `k` that value:
   1. `GenDispatcher::sample` sets each generator's `kw_base` to `max(1/k, cur + p_diff · w_i / W / k)` and its `kvar_base` to `max(0, cur + q_diff · w_i / W / k)`.
   2. The StorageController's power modes (Follow, Support and PeakShave in its discharge path, PeakShaveLow in its charge path) add `kw_needed / k` where they add `kw_needed` to an element's `present_kw` (the `dispatch_kw` and `charge_kw` sums), and add `k · get_fleet_kw` where they add the fleet's kW to `p_diff`.
   3. The current modes (CurrentPeakShave, CurrentPeakShaveLow) and the ESPVLControl stay.
   The mock environments of the class tests keep the default, so their pins hold as they are. Upstream bug, fixed and pinned, report 110.
7. The CapControl `Pf` guards at the balanced total (decision D22): the sample context of `cap_control/control_loop.rs` takes `k` (3 when `positive_sequence` is set, 1 otherwise, the `power_scale` of item 6 or the context's own reading of the flag) and compares `s.im · 0.001` with `k · TotalKvar · PctMinkvar / 100` to close and with `k · TotalKvar / NumSteps · 0.5` to stay closed. Upstream bug, fixed and pinned, report 110.

**Pins** (`exec/tests/make_pos_seq/controls.rs`, every deck on `STIFF`).
