| `a_gang_regulator_selecting_phase_two_keeps_its_tap` | `new transformer.reg phases=3 windings=2 buses=[b r] kvs=[12.47 12.47] kvas=[5000 5000] xhl=0.1 %loadloss=0.01`, `new regcontrol.rc transformer=reg winding=2 vreg=122 band=2 ptratio=60 ptphase=2`, `new load.lr phases=3 bus1=r kv=12.47 kw=3000 pf=0.9 model=2`, `SOLVE`, the tap read, `makeposseq`, a solve | `ptphase 1`, no panic, the tap after the reduction equal to the tap before. The step base indexes `vbuffer[1]` on a buffer of the reduced `nphases` and panics | exact | decision D22, report 110 |
| `a_power_factor_capcontrol_compares_the_balanced_total_with_its_whole_bank` | `new line.l bus1=b bus2=c phases=3 r1=0.1 x1=0.3 length=1`, `new load.ld bus1=c phases=3 kv=12.47 kw=1000 kvar=200 model=2`, `new capacitor.c bus1=c phases=3 kvar=600 kv=12.47 states=[0]`, `new capcontrol.cp element=line.l terminal=1 capacitor=c type=pf onsetting=0.99 offsetting=1.0` (`PctMinkvar` at its default 50), `SOLVE`, the bank's state read, `makeposseq`, a solve | the bank open before and after the reduction: the line's reactive flow of about 200 kvar lies between a third (100 kvar) and the whole (300 kvar) of the guard `TotalKvar · PctMinkvar / 100`, so the unreduced control holds the bank open and the reduced one compares the balanced 200 kvar with `k · 200 · 0.5 = 300` kvar and holds it open too, where the step base compares it with 100 kvar and closes the bank | exact | decision D22, report 110 |
| `a_recloser_on_a_two_phase_line_scales_its_phase_pickups` | `new line.l2 phases=2 bus1=b.1.2 bus2=c.1.2 r1=0.1 x1=0.3 length=1`, `new recloser.rc monitoredobj=line.l2 monitoredterm=1 phasetrip=300 phaseinst=900` | `phasetrip 200`, `phaseinst 600` (`α_I = 2/3`) | T1 | decision D22 |
| `a_current_capcontrol_on_a_two_phase_line_scales_its_ct_ratio` | the same Line, `new capacitor.c phases=2 bus1=c.1.2 kvar=300 kv=12.47`, `new capcontrol.cc element=line.l2 terminal=1 capacitor=c type=current ctratio=60 on=5 off=2` | `ctratio 40`, `on 5`, `off 2` (the settings after the CT stay) | T1 | decision D22 |
| `a_storage_controller_reads_the_balanced_total_of_its_reduced_line` | `new line.l bus1=b bus2=c phases=3 r1=0.1 x1=0.3 length=1`, `new load.ld bus1=c phases=3 kv=12.47 kw=600 pf=1 model=2`, `new storage.st phases=3 bus1=c kv=12.47 kwrated=300 kwhrated=1200 %stored=100`, `new storagecontroller.sc element=line.l terminal=1 kwtarget=900 kwband=60`, `SOLVE`, `storagecontroller.sc.kwneed` read, `makeposseq`, a solve, `kwneed` read again | `kwtarget 900` and `kwband 60` kept (`α_S = 1`). The reading `kwneed + kwtarget` after the reduction equals the one before, the unreduced line power of about 600 kW (T3), and the fleet idles before and after. The step base reads `3 P` after the reduction (`kwneed` near +900) and discharges the fleet | T1, T3 | decision D22, report 110 |
| `a_storage_controller_dispatches_its_fleet_at_the_balanced_total` | `new line.l bus1=b bus2=c phases=3 r1=0 x1=0.1 length=1 units=km`, `new load.ld bus1=c phases=3 kv=12.47 kw=3000 pf=1 model=1`, `new storage.st phases=3 bus1=c kv=12.47 kwrated=1500 kva=1500 kwhrated=6000 %stored=100 %idlingkw=0`, `new storagecontroller.sc element=line.l terminal=1 kwtarget=2500 kwband=10`, `SOLVE`, `makeposseq`, a solve | unreduced: the fleet discharges 500 kW and the line carries 2500 kW. Reduced: `storage.st.kw` 166.667 (the balanced 500 kW over 3), the line power the engine reports 2500 kW, no error. Exact, since the line has no resistance and the load draws constant power on a stiff bus | T1, T3 | decision D22, report 110 |
| `a_gen_dispatcher_settles_its_generator_at_the_balanced_total` | `new line.l bus1=b bus2=c phases=3 r1=0 x1=0.1 length=1 units=km`, `new load.ld bus1=c phases=3 kv=12.47 kw=3000 pf=1 model=1`, `new generator.g bus1=c phases=3 kv=12.47 kw=1000 pf=1 kva=5000`, `new gendispatcher.gd element=line.l terminal=1 kwlimit=1500 kwband=10`, `SOLVE`, `makeposseq`, a solve | unreduced: `generator.g.kw` 1500, the line 1500 kW. Reduced: `kwlimit 1500` and `kwband 10` kept, `generator.g.kw` 500 (the balanced 1500 kW over 3), the line power the engine reports 1500 kW, no error. The step base jumps between 1 and 1498 kW per phase and ends at the control-iteration limit (the executor records its message) | T1, T3 | decision D22, report 110 |
| `a_recloser_reads_no_residual_on_a_positive_sequence_circuit` | `new line.l bus1=b bus2=c phases=3 r1=0.1 x1=0.3 length=1`, `new load.ld bus1=c phases=3 kv=12.47 kw=6000 pf=0.9 model=2` (about 309 A), `new recloser.rc monitoredobj=line.l monitoredterm=1 phasetrip=1000 groundtrip=100`, `makeposseq`, a solve | the residual the recloser compares with `groundtrip` reads 0 where the step base reads the one conductor's current (about 309 A), and the recloser does not operate | exact | decision D22 |
| `l13_a_control_on_a_coupled_element_takes_its_first_circuits_factor` (L13) | the deck of MPS-04's `limits::l1_circuits_of_different_phase_count`, `new recloser.rc monitoredobj=line.d2 monitoredterm=1 phasetrip=300` | `phasetrip 300` (circuit 1, `N_c = 3`), so circuit 2 (`N_c = 2`, `α_I = 2/3`) trips at 1.5 times its current | T1 | stated limit L13 |

**Divergences.** `modes:makeposseq/makeposseq_ctrl.dss` on both channels: the planner measures the props and solved cells of every control whose setting the step rescales (the deck holds a wye-winding RegControl, whose factors are 1, and the controls the audit lists) and of the StorageController `stc`, which monitors `line.l1` reduced to one phase, so the reader of item 5 and the dispatch of item 6 move its reading and its fleet `st1`. One entry per moved field group, with the cause `makeposseq-keeps-control-settings` for a rescaled setting and `storage-controller-reads-and-dispatches-at-the-balanced-total` for `stc` and `st1`, each held by the pin of its class above, and the step stops if a value moves that the audit does not explain. The new entries that scope `powers`, `losses` or deck-wide `voltages` move the two registers of `corpus_gate/ledger.rs`. `exec::tests::element_extras::makeposseq_does_not_reattach_controls` and `exec::tests::make_pos_seq::makeposseq_control_after_target_sees_converted_target` keep their rule and are checked to pass.

**Upstream report.** Report 110: "MakePosSequence leaves control settings at the values of the multi-phase circuit, and a control on a positive-sequence circuit reads its one conductor's current as the residual", measured on r4133 with epri-worker on the decks of `a_regulator_between_two_phases_keeps_its_tap` and `a_recloser_reads_no_residual_on_a_positive_sequence_circuit`, with a section "the CapControl power-factor guards compare the balanced total with a third of the bank, and a phase selector above the reduced count reads the wrong conductor" (the decks of `a_power_factor_capcontrol_compares_the_balanced_total_with_its_whole_bank` and `a_voltage_capcontrol_selecting_phase_three_reads_the_reduced_conductor`, measured on r4133 with epri-worker) and two more sections: "the StorageController reads nine times the power of a one-phase element on a positive-sequence circuit" (the deck of `a_storage_controller_reads_the_balanced_total_of_its_reduced_line`) and "the GenDispatcher and the StorageController add a balanced-total deficit to a reduced element's own power" (the deck of `a_gen_dispatcher_settles_its_generator_at_the_balanced_total`), each measured on r4133 with epri-worker.

**Parts.**
- P1: the class audit, `controls.rs`, `ControlTarget`, RegControl, its three pins. Commit.
- P2: CapControl, Relay, Recloser, Fuse settings, the phase selectors of item 3, the `Pf` guards of item 7, the residual and negative-sequence reading of item 4, their pins. Commit.
- P3: the StorageController reader of item 5, the dispatch of item 6 in GenDispatcher and StorageController, their three pins. Commit.
- P4: ESPVLControl, UPFCControl, InvControl, ExpControl, SwtControl and the meters by the audit, the L13 pin. Commit.
- P5: the corpus measurement, ledger, the two registers, lock, props rows. Commit.
- P6: DIVERGENCES entry, record (with the audit table), STATUS, report 110. Commit.

**Acceptance.** Every pin passes in both lanes and fails at the step base (an unscaled ratio or pickup, the tap at its limit, nine times the reduced power read, a dispatch that moves three times its deficit, the residual read as the phase current, a selector above the count that panics or reads 0 V, a `Pf` guard at a third), and the L13 pin holds the stated limit. The audit table is in the record: every setting it lists as read through the target is rescaled or named with the reason it stays, every phase selector it lists reads conductor 1 on a reduced target of fewer conductors, every comparison of a reading with the element's own rating it lists converts by `k`, and every power reader it lists gives the balanced total on a positive-sequence circuit. The corpus gate is green with every ledger entry hit, no golden byte moved unless the step lists it.

## 5. Order and dependencies

| wave | steps (lanes) | waits for | why these share a wave |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | MPS-01 | nothing | the pipeline and the legs every later step plugs into |
| 2 | MPS-02, MPS-07 | MPS-01 | MPS-02 edits `exec/make_pos_seq/`, `elements/pos_seq/` (`mod.rs`, `target/`), `circuit/circuit.rs`, the transformer leg files and `exec/tests/make_pos_seq/map.rs`, MPS-07 edits the Capacitor and Reactor files, `support/cmatrix` and the fixture of `solution/ymatrix.rs` tests: disjoint |
| 3 | MPS-03, MPS-08 | MPS-02 and MPS-07 (MPS-03), MPS-02 (MPS-08) | MPS-03 edits the PD classes, `exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs`, `elements/pos_seq/project.rs`, `target/line.rs`, `target/lumped.rs`, `elements/traits.rs`, `projection.rs` and `limits.rs` of the tests, MPS-08 the PC and transformer classes, `exec/make_pos_seq/pc.rs`, `target/pc.rs`, `target/winding.rs` and `pc.rs` of the tests: disjoint |
| 4 | MPS-04, MPS-06 | MPS-03 and MPS-08 (both) | MPS-04 edits `merge.rs`, the two `project.rs`, `apply.rs`, the PD writes, `elements/ckt.rs`, `target/line.rs`, `target/lumped.rs`, `coupled.rs` and `limits.rs`, MPS-06 the source classes, `sources.rs`, `target/source.rs` and `sources.rs` of the tests: disjoint |
| 5 | MPS-05, MPS-10 | MPS-04 (both), MPS-08 (MPS-10) | MPS-05 edits `chains.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs`, `exec/make_pos_seq/pc.rs`, `target/pc.rs`, `target/lumped.rs`, `chains.rs` and `limits.rs` of the tests, MPS-10 `controls.rs`, `target/control.rs`, the control classes, `solution/controls/dispatch.rs` and `controls.rs` of the tests: disjoint. MPS-05 is the only step of the wave that changes the elimination |
| 6 | MPS-09 | every step | the whole procedure |

- One lane per step, within the machine ceiling (about three workflows and three to four building lanes). Every launch follows the checklist of section 3.
- Single-lane rule: a step that changes the node map (`exec/make_pos_seq/map.rs`, `ports.rs`), the elimination (`exec/make_pos_seq/project.rs` item 3 of MPS-03, `chains.rs`) or `solution/ymatrix.rs` beyond a test fixture runs in no wave with another such step. MPS-02, MPS-03, MPS-04 and MPS-05 are such steps and run in waves 2, 3, 4 and 5.
- Shared documents: `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, `docs/upgrade/DIVERGENCES.md`, `docs/phase-records/retro-fixes.md`, `STATUS.md` and the props pin files `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_pins.rs` and `props_r4133_replay.rs` take changes from two lanes of a wave. Each step adds its own ledger entries, its own DIVERGENCES entry under its own heading and its own record block, edits only the props rows of the elements it reduces, and never edits another step's lines. Three files are never merged by hand: `tests/corpus/manifests/population.lock.json` and the two registers of `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs` (`AGGREGATE_VALUE_ARMS_INHERITING_THE_ELEMENT_LEDGER`, a fixed-length array, and `BUS_ARRAY_SUPPRESSIONS`), with the population rails of a step that adds a case. The second landing of a wave rebases, regenerates the lock (`python tmp/retro_fix/bin/gatelock.py --label "<tag> lock" -- cargo test -p dss-core --test population_lock` with `DSS_UPDATE_POPULATION_LOCK=1`), re-derives both registers and the rails from the merged ledger and manifest, and runs the full gate (the update moved).
- Queue rows replaced: row 13 by MPS-01 to MPS-06, MPS-09 and MPS-10, row 15 by MPS-07, row 16 by MPS-08. Those rows of `RULINGS_QUEUE.md` point to this plan.
- Outside the plan: queue steps 2 (a rating that is not set) and 4 (LineGeometry rules) are on `update`. Queue steps 10 to 12 (rating details) may land before or after, and a step of this plan that runs after them follows their rating states, and a class that gains a solve refusal there implements `pos_seq_refusal` (case 6). Queue steps 14 (LineGeometry additions, running), 17 (unit words, `nconds=` in LineSpacing) and 21 (the `nconds` default) edit the Line's geometry and spacing path and its tests (`pd/line/solve.rs`, `pd/line/tests.rs`), the files MPS-03 and MPS-04 edit: none of them shares a wave with MPS-03 or MPS-04, and MPS-03 launches after step 14 has landed. Queue step 5 (a VCCS without curves refused) edits `pc/vccs`, so it does not share a wave with MPS-08. Queue step 20 (the Capacitor delta and `cmatrix` filter stamp) edits `Capacitor::make_yprim_work` and must not share a wave with MPS-03, MPS-04 or MPS-07. A queue step outside the plan that owes a report takes a number above the block this plan reserves (section 0).

## 6. Stated limits (L1 to L6, and L7 to L13 of decision D20)

A limit is pinned with its size, never refused. Sizes below are the numpy measurements of section 1.16. Each pin re-measures in the engine (T5 unless named).

| limit | size | pin | step |
|---|---|---|---|
| L1. Only the symmetric part of an inter-circuit term is written. It is exact for two circuits in the same phase order at a displacement of 0 or 180 degrees whose Kron-reduced inter-circuit block `B` satisfies `B13 - B31 = (B12 - B21) + (B23 - B32)` (`Z1[1,2] - Z1[2,1] = (j/√3)(D13 - D12 - D23)` with `D = B - Bᵀ`, so a symmetric block is the common sufficient case). Otherwise it loses the reactive power `6 Re(A12) Im(conj(i1) i2)` (with `A12` real up to the earth-resistance terms, whose imaginary part moves `6 Im(A12) Im(conj(i1) i2)` of active power) and part of the voltage effect: for a displacement `θ` of circuit II other than 0 or 180 degrees (a delta-wye bank, or a geometric roll of its conductors by one position, which acts as 120 degrees) it writes `m cos θ` of the coupling `m` and loses `m sin θ` as `A12`, `ρ = |tan θ|`, so the written mutual of the rolled Kersting pole is minus one half of its phased-alike value, for a mirrored phase order (not exact even on a mirror-symmetric tower), for circuits of different phase count and for an unsymmetric block. The active power is kept to the line losses' change | Kersting Example 4.2 (rolled, `ρ = 1.732`): 4.7e-3 (-36.6 kvar, -0.87 kW) and 8.05e-3 (-50.3 kvar, -0.64 kW) of the source power under constant-power loads, 4.7e-5 at equal power factors. Phased alike on the same pole `ρ = 3.1e-5`. Line 2 on two phases `ρ = 6.67`, 5.0e-4. Tleis Example 3.4 RYB/BYR `ρ = 0.158`. Behind a delta-wye bank the written mutual is `cos 30°` of the true one, `ρ = 0.577` | `limits::l1_a_rolled_double_circuit_loses_reactive_power`, `limits::l1_a_circuit_behind_a_delta_wye_bank_writes_cos_30_of_its_mutual`, `limits::l1_circuits_of_different_phase_count`, `a_double_circuit_phased_alike_has_a_nearly_reciprocal_mutual`, `tleis_3_4_writes_the_symmetric_part_of_the_rolled_mutual` | MPS-04 |
| L2. A one- or two-phase element on a three-phase network is off in its shunt impedance by `(Z2 + Z0) / (3z)` (one phase to earth) and `Z2 / z` (between two phases), the exact shunt being `3z + Z2 + Z0` and `z + Z2`. The source power moves by `(2 Re[(3z + Z1) conj(Z2 + Z0)] + |Z2 + Z0|²) / |3z + Z1|²`, up to about twice `|Z2 + Z0| / |3z|` | a 100 ohm resistor from one phase to earth behind `Z1 = 0.2 + j0.6`, `Z0 = 0.5 + j1.5` ohm: 4.7e-3 of the source power (the shunt impedance 7.4e-3 off), a 100 ohm reactor 1.4e-2, `30 + j40` ohm 2.8e-2 against `2 |Z2 + Z0| / |3z| = 3.0e-2` | `limits::a_one_phase_element_is_off_by_the_other_sequences_of_the_network` | MPS-03 |
| L3. A two-phase Line serving a load between its phases is off by `|Z11 + Z22 - 2 Z12 - (3/4)(Z11 + Z22 - Z12)| / |z_load|`. A two-phase lateral has no balanced state: balanced currents on its two coupled conductors split the end voltages, which the reduced circuit reads at their mean, so a voltage-reading control on the lateral (MPS-10) sees the mean of two voltages that differ by that split | Kersting 4.1 lateral, 5 mi, 1000 kW between the phases: 2.3e-3, loop form 3.0e-5. Two 500 kW loads to earth: end voltages 7007.6 and 6847.0 V around the reduced 6926.2 V, `±80` V (1.2 per cent), the power 3.4e-4 | `limits::a_load_between_the_phases_of_a_lateral_is_off_by_the_loop_difference` | MPS-04 |
| L4. Unbalanced loads have no positive-sequence form | P-4W with loads to earth of 1300, 1000, 700 kW: 6.8e-3 | `limits::l4_unbalanced_loads_have_no_positive_sequence_form` | MPS-05 |
| L5. A three-phase fault behind an unsymmetric series element is off by the gap of the two forms, 1 to 4 per cent on an untransposed line or a flat cable and 0.7 per cent on the stacked reactor, on its positive-sequence current, and its phase currents spread around the reduced one | Kersting 4.1 line 1.1e-2 on the complex currents (2063.6 A against `I1` of 2078.3 A on the P-4W deck, magnitudes 7.0e-3 apart), the unreduced phase currents +1.1e-1, -4.2e-2 and -3.3e-2 from the reduced one, Tleis 3.4 one circuit 1.4e-2, stacked reactor 6.6e-3, Tleis 3.5 solidly bonded cable 3.7e-2 (`0.04299 + j0.12878` against `0.03917 + j0.12547` ohm/km) | `limits::a_fault_behind_an_unsymmetric_line_is_off_by_the_form_gap` (MPS-03), `limits::l5_the_solidly_bonded_cable_forms_differ_by_four_per_cent` (MPS-05, T1) | MPS-03, MPS-05 |
| L6. A fault at the end of a one- or two-phase lateral is off by more than 10 per cent | the two-phase lateral, a bolted fault of both phases to earth: 1.3e-1 (1501.7 A against 1724.6 A) | `limits::a_fault_at_the_end_of_a_two_phase_lateral_is_off_by_more_than_ten_per_cent` | MPS-03 |
| L7. The form rule is static: it reads the node map, not the solved state | the shunt stacked reactor on a star made a port by 0.001 ohm to earth: 6.6e-3 where the admittance form is exact | `limits::the_form_rule_reads_the_map_not_the_solved_state` | MPS-03 |
| L8. A tap at a joint inside a cable chain: the chain carries one current, so the K2 shares keep the core losses and drop the sheath losses that the unequal section currents induce | the Tleis three-section cable with 40 per cent of the load at the first joint: 3.2e-5, the cable loss 4.7e-2 low (2018.7 W against 2119.0 W, the 100.4 W of 4 to 8 A circulating in the sheaths) | `limits::l8_a_tap_inside_a_cross_bonded_chain` | MPS-05 |
| L9. A power conversion or lumped conductor at a chain node reads it at zero volts | P-4W with its neutral earthed at one end and three loads to the far neutral: balanced 6.3e-5 (the neutral's induced drop), 1300, 1000, 700 kW 9.9e-3 | `a_balanced_shared_neutral_is_read_at_zero_volts`, `limits::l9_unequal_line_to_neutral_loads_on_a_line_neutral` | MPS-05 |
| L10. A coupled `phases=M` element holds one rating, the smallest of its circuits', so a stronger circuit reads its loading high | a three-phase and a two-phase circuit of equal conductors: circuit 1 reads 1.5 times its loading. Kersting 4.2 with 530 A and 329 A conductors would read circuit 1 61 per cent high | `limits::l10_a_coupled_element_holds_one_rating` (T1) | MPS-04 |
| L11. Legs at different per-unit voltages in the balanced state (a two-phase delta element with a leg to earth, an element on a joint star with others) are written by the nominal admittance at the largest leg's voltage: exact for constant impedance and for `kvar` elements, off for another load model, and the written power is not the rated power over 3 (decision D17 weighs the candidate at the RMS voltage of the legs) | a two-phase delta constant-power load, its legs at 1.0 and 0.577 pu above `vminpu=0.5`: 3.3e-1 (400 against 600 kW) | `l11_a_constant_power_two_phase_delta_load_is_off_by_a_third` | MPS-08 |
| L12. An element whose own stamp is not balanced (an open-delta transformer bank) has no positive-sequence form beyond its projection: the joint projection of section 1.13 keeps the relation of the balanced-current state (`2z/3` for an equal pair), while under load the bank puts a negative-sequence voltage on its secondary that the reduced circuit cannot hold, the two reduced units sit in parallel so that taps which differ between them circulate a current the real bank does not carry (its units feed different phases), and a multi-phase element between the same two ports is its own set in parallel | the open-delta pair of section 1.16 with 1000 kW of delta load: 4.7e-4 (the two units at `z/2` 1.1e-2, the gap of the two forms of L5 on a set that carries balanced currents), the negative-sequence voltage 2.2 per cent, the IEEE 37 pair with its jumper 1.6e-5 at taps 1.00 to 1.06 | `an_open_delta_pair_is_projected_as_one_set_at_two_thirds_of_a_unit`, `an_open_delta_regulator_pair_with_its_jumper_is_projected_as_one_set` | MPS-08 |
| L13. A control on a coupled element holds one setting for circuits whose readings scale by different factors (decision D22): it takes the first circuit's factor, so another circuit reads high or low by the ratio of the factors | a recloser on a three-phase and a two-phase circuit: the two-phase circuit trips at 1.5 times its current | `l13_a_control_on_a_coupled_element_takes_its_first_circuits_factor` (T1) | MPS-10 |

Note on L1: the symmetric part is exact for two circuits in the same phase order at a displacement of 0 or 180 degrees when `B13 - B31 = (B12 - B21) + (B23 - B32)`, which a symmetric Kron-reduced inter-circuit block satisfies. Kersting's pole phased alike gives `ρ = 3.1e-5` because its neutral sits nearer line 2 and leaves `B` unsymmetric (`max |B - Bᵀ| = 0.0063` ohm/mile, `ρ = 6e-17` with the neutral struck), and Tleis's RYB/RYB tower gives 0 because its earth wire sits on the tower axis (`max |B - Bᵀ| = 3.5e-18`, rounding noise: the printed data make the block symmetric, `x15 = x24`, `x16 = x34`, `x26 = x35` with equal earth-wire terms). A mirrored phase order is not an exact case: on the same tower RYB/BYR gives `Z1[1,2] = -0.00224 - j0.01318` and `Z1[2,1] = 0.00193 - j0.01332` ohm/km, `ρ = 0.158` (Tleis Example 3.4, p. 139, prints `-0.002 - j0.013` and `0.002 - j0.013`), since for a mirrored column the two directions are `(1/3) vᴴ B v̄` and `(1/3) vᵀ B v`, equal only when `B22 - B33 = 2 (B12 - B13)`, an accident of geometry that the tower does not meet, and Clarke Vol. I p. 420 states that in the sequence networks the mutual impedances "are not, in general, reciprocal". The displacement rule `ρ = |tan θ|` for a symmetric block gives the plan's 0.577 behind a delta-wye bank and 1.732 on the rolled pole (a roll by one position is a displacement of 120 degrees). The plan pins the measured sizes.

## 7. Coverage

## 8. Questions for the user, decided

The plan put four questions to the user, and each was decided on 2026-10-10 with the plan's proposal under the user's rule of that date (the head of section 2), so nothing stays open and every step builds what is written here.

- **Q1. A two-phase Line whose loads all lie between its two phases (decision D7).** Candidates: (a) the 120-degree form `(3/4)(Z11 + Z22 - Z12)` of section 1.8 with stated limit L3, (b) the loop form `Z11 + Z22 - 2 Z12` when every element downstream of the Line lies between its phases, which equals the rule of IEEE Std 551 section 3.7, p. 68, for uncoupled conductors on a stiff source (the standard counts the source impedance twice, the reduced circuit once). On a 5-mile lateral of Kersting Example 4.1's conductors a and c with its neutral and one 1000 kW load between the phases, (a) misses the source power by 2.3e-3 and (b) by 3.0e-5. With two 500 kW loads to earth (a) misses by 3.4e-4 and (b) does not apply. (b) needs a scan of everything downstream of the Line and is wrong as soon as one load to earth is added. Decided: (a), with L3 pinned by MPS-04.
- **Q2. A non-reciprocal inter-circuit term (decision D5).** Candidates: (a) the symmetric part always, the books' practice and the user's word of 2026-10-04, (b) a refusal when `ρ = |A12| / |S12|` exceeds a share. Kersting Example 4.2 (two lines on one pole, phases rolled) has `ρ = 1.732` with 4.7e-3 and 8.05e-3 of the source power lost in reactive power under constant-power loads, Tleis Example 3.4 RYB/BYR `ρ = 0.158`, and a three-phase and a two-phase circuit on the Kersting pole `ρ = 6.67` with only 5.0e-4 of the source power off. The lost power depends on the load currents, which the command does not know, and a share that refuses Kersting 4.2 refuses that ordinary pair as well. Decided: (a), no refusal, with L1 pinned by MPS-04.
- **Q3. The four-conductor line with its neutral retained (open question 8 of step 3, decision D21).** Decided as section 1.5 states: the neutral on node 0 at both ends is Kron-reduced in `Z` and struck in `C`. On Kersting Example 4.1's line, 5 miles at 12.47 kV with 3000 + j1500 kVA, this keeps the source power to 6.4e-5 where the mean over four conductors misses it by 8.3e-3 and puts the line loss 21 per cent high. MPS-03 builds it.
- **Q4. An element whose terminal sees its port in negative order (section 1.4 item 3, decision D11).** Candidates: (a) the order check for every source, winding and power conversion element, (b) the check only for the elements whose model depends on the order. A balanced Load, a passive bank and a winding draw the same balanced power in either order, `Y1 = (1/3) Σ_k |Δw_k|² y`, so (a) refuses circuits the reduction keeps exactly. Decided: (b), as section 1.4 item 3 and section 1.12 case 2 state it. The check covers Vsource, Isource, VSConverter, IndMach012, and a three-phase PVSystem, Storage or `model=7` Generator with `Balanced=yes`, each against the pattern its model imposes (section 1.4 item 3, refusal case 2). MPS-02 builds it with the refusal pins `an_induction_machine_on_a_negative_order_port_stops_makeposseq`, `a_projected_vsource_on_a_negative_order_port_stops_makeposseq`, `a_two_phase_isource_on_phases_one_and_three_stops_makeposseq` and `a_balanced_pvsystem_on_a_negative_order_port_stops_makeposseq`, and `a_load_on_a_negative_order_port_is_reduced_on_its_port` and `a_winding_on_a_negative_order_port_carries_the_reversed_order` hold the elements the check does not refuse.

Items (1) to (9) of the step-3 row are decided by decision D19 and are not questions.

## 9. Out of scope

- Ratings of other classes (queue steps 10 to 12), among them the default `NormAmps` and `EmergAmps` of a `Cuf` Capacitor (`1e6 x phases` too small, report 95) and the leg-current `NormAmps` of a delta bank. A reduced bank inherits whatever those steps decide.
- LineGeometry (queue steps 4, 14, 17 to 19 and 21), with the tape lap (queue step 19) and the earth-return constant of the simple Carson model, a precision item of the parity teardown.
- The Capacitor delta and `cmatrix` filter stamp (queue step 20, section 1.13).
- What a two-phase `z1` Reactor should stamp (a language question). The reduction projects whatever it stamps.
- Dynamics and harmonics, among them the dynamic-model inputs of a WindGen (`Pss`, `Qss`, the turbine data). MakePosSequence is a power-flow model conversion.
- The `ForceBalanced` filter of PVSystem and Storage (`force_pos_seq`), which is not part of the command.
- The step-3 items off the path: a `z1` Reactor with a zero sequence impedance and a series matrix Reactor in a GIC solve stamping a tiny conductance in silence.
- The parity lane teardown, a new DSS syntax, and any class or command that section 1 does not name beyond what section 1.12 reaches through decision D10 and what decision D22 asks of the controls on a reduced circuit.
